Existing methods typically fall into two extremes: voxel-based fusion provides explicit geometric alignment but requires costly 3D construction and class-specific assumptions, whereas lightweight patch-based fusion is efficient but relies on discrete candidate matching and lacks continuous cross-view correspondence. In this paper, we propose GeoMAD, a unified multi-view, multi-class AD framework that addresses both geometric correspondence deficiency and distributional inconsistency. Our \textit{Cross-view Deformable Fusion Module} (CDFM) learns content-adaptive, view-pair-specific sampling offsets directly on 2D feature maps and arranges them across a multi-scale window pyramid with image-global reference sampling, enabling hierarchical cross-view correspondence without camera calibration, voxel construction, or class-specific 3D supervision. We further introduce \textit{Distributional View Alignment} (DVA), a self-supervised cross-view regularization loss that aligns each view's bottleneck distribution against a per-instance view-centric target, enforcing global consistency without pixel-level correspondence. Together, CDFM and DVA bridge local geometric correspondence and global distributional consistency, providing geometry-aware and distribution-consistent fusion while preserving the efficiency of 2D feature-space learning. Extensive experiments on Real-IAD and MANTA-Tiny show that GeoMAD achieves strong detection and localization performance in unified MvAD.

</details>


### [36] [Generative Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26737)
*Shi Chen,Weifeng Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式语义场景完成（GSSC）框架，通过离散扩散模型解决户外LiDAR语义场景完成中的类别不平衡问题，在SemanticKITTI数据集上取得38.8% mIoU的SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外LiDAR语义场景完成面临观测数据稀疏（仅1%体积）和类别不平衡（超过7000倍）的挑战，现有方法难以有效处理长尾分布问题。

Method: 1) 配对稀疏-密集场景合成（PS³）生成匹配的稀疏LiDAR观测和密集语义完成数据；2) 语义引导生成式场景完成（SGSC）通过多项式离散扩散从噪声生成场景；3) 结构化源离散扩散（S²D²）在单步流匹配中优化现有完成结果。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI隐藏测试集上，单步无测试时增强达到38.8% mIoU，比之前最佳结果提升2.1个百分点；四步校正加八视角测试时增强达到39.2% mIoU。

Conclusion: GSSC框架有效解决了类别不平衡问题，S²D²模块可提升任何SSC基线模型性能而无需重新训练，实现了因果、单次扫描、单样本条件下的最佳结果。

Abstract: Outdoor LiDAR semantic scene completion (SSC) recovers a dense semantic voxel grid from a scan observing 1% of the target volume, under class imbalance beyond 7,000x. We recast SSC as generative semantic scene completion (GSSC): a single discrete-diffusion formulation in three roles. First, paired sparse-dense scene synthesis (PS$^3$) generates matched sparse LiDAR observations with their dense semantic completions, addressing the long tail at its source and yielding the PS$^3$-SemanticKITTI corpus we train on alongside SemanticKITTI. Second, semantic-guided generative scene completion (SGSC) generates the scene from noise with multinomial discrete diffusion, conditioned on the sparse scan through a bird's-eye-view semantic map and a sparse 3D feature stream. Third, the same framework instead refines an existing completion in one flow-matching step: structured source discrete diffusion (S$^2$D$^2$). S$^2$D$^2$ improves the mIoU of SGSC's own output and every external SSC base tested, without base retraining or test-time adaptation. On the strongest base, one step without test-time augmentation reaches 38.8% mIoU on the SemanticKITTI hidden test. To our knowledge that is the best causal, single-sweep, single-sample result on that leaderboard, +2.1 pp over the previous best published score under the same restriction. Four correction steps with eight-view test-time augmentation reach 39.2%, outside that restriction.

</details>


### [37] [G2D: Generative-to-Discriminative Collaborative Inference for Zero-Shot Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26744)
*Zehua Hao,Fang Liu,Qinliang Wang,Yaoyang Du,Xinyan Huang,Puhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G2D，一种无需训练的框架，通过结合CLIP的候选检索能力与生成式VLM的细粒度验证能力，解决零样本分类中判别式与生成式模型的互补缺陷，显著提升了分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本分类需要高效的标签检索和细粒度的视觉推理，但现有的判别式模型（如CLIP）和生成式视觉语言模型（VLM）存在互补性缺陷：CLIP在Top-1预测错误时，正确标签常保留在Top-K列表中，主要挑战在于消歧而非召回；而独立的生成式模型受限于巨大的标签空间和无约束的输出，难以直接应用。

Method: 提出G2D框架，将广泛的候选检索与细粒度的图像接地验证分离。利用生成式VLM对CLIP检索到的候选项进行验证，利用候选名称和CLIP概率作为结构化先验。采用固定置信度路由、熵自适应候选集大小调整以及Trie约束解码，将生成式推理聚焦于不确定样本，并确保测试时每个输入仅产生一个有效输出。

Result: 在八个基准测试中，G2D平均准确率达到68.85%，显著优于CLIP的59.35%和独立VLM的63.11%。在七种生成器配置下，候选集验证使平均准确率提升了1.08至27.42个百分点。该框架还可迁移至DCLIP、WaffleCLIP和CuPL等模型。

Conclusion: G2D成功构建了判别式提案与生成式视觉推理之间的实用接口，证明了通过分离检索与验证阶段，可以有效利用两类模型的互补优势，在无需额外训练的情况下显著提升零样本分类性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot classification needs efficient label retrieval and fine-grained visual reasoning, yet discriminative and generative vision-language models fail in complementary ways.When CLIP's top-1 prediction is wrong, the correct label often remains in its top-$K$ shortlist, making disambiguation rather than recall the key challenge.Standalone generative models, however, are hindered by large label spaces and unconstrained outputs.This complementarity motivates separating broad candidate retrieval from fine-grained, image-grounded verification.We propose G2D, a training-free framework that uses a generative VLM to verify CLIP-retrieved candidates against the image.Candidate names and CLIP probabilities provide a structured prior for resolving visually similar classes.Fixed confidence routing, entropy-adaptive candidate sizing, and trie-constrained decoding focus generative reasoning on uncertain samples and ensure one valid output for each input at test time.Across eight benchmarks, G2D achieves 68.85% average accuracy, versus 59.35% for CLIP and 63.11% for the standalone VLM.Across seven generator configurations, candidate-set verification improves average accuracy by 1.08--27.42 percentage points.G2D also transfers to DCLIP, WaffleCLIP, and CuPL, supporting a practical interface between discriminative proposal and generative visual reasoning. Code: https://github.com/Harzva/G2D

</details>


### [38] [Glass Surface Detection Grounded in 3D Visual Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26752)
*Yiwei Lu,Ke Xu,Tao Yan,Xiaojun Chang,Radu Timofte,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D视觉几何的玻璃表面检测（GSD）新范式，通过从VGGT中提取3D先验并设计包含频率自注意力模块（FSAM）和几何基础块（GeGB）的新型检测头，解决了传统2D方法在几何模糊场景中的失效问题，在七个基准测试中实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃表面检测对场景理解和重建至关重要，但由于玻璃的透明性和反射性，现有依赖2D外观线索的方法在几何模糊场景中往往失效，因此需要一种能显式建模玻璃物理存在的新方法。

Method: 首先从视觉几何基础变压器（VGGT）中提取丰富的3D先验以生成玻璃感知的3D表示；随后利用多任务学习框架，结合新型玻璃检测头进行训练，该检测头包含两个核心模块：用于定位玻璃表面的频率自注意力模块（FSAM）和用于分割玻璃表面的几何基础块（GeGB），后者能将2D特征选择性地基础化到3D几何中。

Result: 在七个标准玻璃表面检测基准测试中取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能，展现出对视频和多模态数据的良好泛化能力，并显著提升了玻璃场景下的重建质量。

Conclusion: 将玻璃表面检测基础化于3D视觉几何的范式转变是有效的，该方法不仅克服了传统2D方法的局限性，还为复杂场景下的玻璃表面精确检测与重建提供了强有力的解决方案。

Abstract: Glass surface detection (GSD) is critical for scene understanding and reconstruction, and yet remains challenging due to the transparency and reflectivity of glass surfaces. Existing GSD methods typically rely on 2D appearance cues, which may fail in geometrically ambiguous scenes. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift: grounding GSD in 3D visual geometry to explicitly model the physical existence of glass surfaces. Our method first distills rich 3D priors from the visual geometry grounded transformer (VGGT) and generates glass-aware 3D representations. It then exploits multi-tasking learning with a novel glass detection head, consisting of two core modules: a Frequency Self-Attention Module (FSAM) that identifies glass-specific spectral features for glass surface localization, and a Geometry Grounding Block (GeGB) that selectively grounds 2D features in 3D geometry for glass surface segmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across seven standard GSD benchmarks, generalizes well to video/multi-modal data, and substantially improves reconstruction in glass scenes. Code is available in https://github.com/YT3DVision/VGGT_GLASS.

</details>


### [39] [Cross-Architecture Knowledge Distillation from a Vision Foundation Model to a Lightweight Visual State Space Model for Tea Leaf Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26771)
*Zibo Zhou,Zongsen Qiu,Rui Chen,Yujie Yao,Yue Zhou,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨架构知识蒸馏方法，将大型DINOv2视觉Transformer教师模型的知识迁移至轻量级双向视觉状态空间模型（LVSSM）学生模型，成功解决了在有限农业数据集上训练SSM的稳定性问题，实现了在参数量减少5倍的情况下，茶叶病害分类准确率提升3.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化茶叶病害分类对精准农业至关重要，但在计算资源受限的边缘设备上部署高精度模型仍具挑战性。现有的自监督视觉基础模型（如DINOv2）特征强大但体积过大，而从头训练的小型模型在小型农业数据集上容易欠拟合，因此需要一种能兼顾精度与效率的解决方案。

Method: 研究采用跨架构知识蒸馏（KD），将微调后的DINOv2（Vision Transformer）教师模型的知识蒸馏至紧凑的bidirectional Visual State Space Model (LVSSM) 学生模型。针对SSM在小数据上训练不稳定的问题，提出了两项改进：引入渐进式卷积茎（progressive convolutional stem）替代单一的大patch嵌入卷积，以及设计门控双向选择性扫描块（gated bidirectional selective-scan block）以修复切断残差路径的融合层问题。最终采用温度缩放的对数蒸馏（logit distillation）进行训练。

Result: 学生模型参数量仅为4.45M（教师模型的1/5），但在三个随机种子下的测试准确率从92.32%±2.14%提升至95.41%±1.17%（最佳单次运行96.20%），宏观F1分数达94.45%，保留了教师模型98.3%的准确率。消融实验表明，简单的对数级蒸馏优于中间特征对齐损失。

Conclusion: 跨架构知识蒸馏能有效提升轻量级SSM在小型农业数据集上的表现，特别是在学生模型初始性能低于教师模型时增益显著。该方法在大幅降低参数量和计算成本的同时，实现了高精度的茶叶病害分类，但仍受限于单一数据集范围及非官方SSM实现的简化。

Abstract: Automated tea leaf disease classification supports precision agriculture, yet deploying accurate models on edge devices remains challenging under tight compute budgets. Self-supervised vision foundation models such as DINOv2 provide strong features but are too large for field deployment, while lightweight models trained from scratch on small agricultural datasets often underfit. We study cross-architecture knowledge distillation (KD) from a fine-tuned DINOv2 teacher (Vision Transformer) to a compact bidirectional Visual State Space Model (LVSSM) student, an underexplored direction because the architectures use fundamentally different token-mixing mechanisms. We identify and fix two training-stability problems that prevent the from-scratch SSM student from learning on limited data: a single large patch-embedding convolution and a fusion layer that severs the residual path. With a progressive convolutional stem and gated bidirectional selective-scan block, the 4.45M-parameter student trains stably. Across three seeds, temperature-scaled logit distillation raises test accuracy from 92.32+/-2.14% to 95.41+/-1.17% (best single run: 96.20%; macro-F1: 94.45%), a +3.09 percentage-point mean gain. The student uses 5.0 times fewer parameters than the 22M-parameter teacher while retaining 98.3% of its accuracy. Ablations show that intermediate feature-alignment losses reduce accuracy, making simple logit-level KD the strongest configuration. A fair from-scratch comparison shows the gain is specific to students that start below the teacher. We report per-class metrics, confusion matrices, bootstrap confidence intervals, and FLOPs/latency measurements, and discuss limitations including the single-dataset scope and simplified non-official SSM implementation.

</details>

### [40] [TreeGraft: Adaptive Multi-Drafter Grafting for Tree-Based Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26112)
*Jiaming Fan,Daming Cao,Canchen Huang,Jiale Fu,Jin Zhang,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang,Xiangzhong Luo,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeGraft 提出了一种多草稿模型框架，通过让不同成本的草稿模型协同构建共享草稿树，并利用轻量级调度器动态调用强草稿模型进行重评分和路径恢复，解决了单草稿模型在速度与质量间的权衡难题，显著提升了大语言模型的推测解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的树结构推测解码方法通常仅使用单一草稿模型，面临两难困境：小型草稿模型速度快但生成的树质量低，大型草稿模型虽能提升树质量却带来高延迟，限制了推理加速效果。

Method: 提出 TreeGraft 多草稿框架，利用强草稿模型对弱草稿模型生成的候选进行重评分、重新选择嫁接位置并恢复被遗漏的有潜力路径，同时非破坏性地整合强模型的扩展以保留潜在可接受分支；此外，引入一个从离线价值系统蒸馏而来的轻量级调度器，动态决策何时调用强草稿模型以控制成本。

Result: 在 10 组模型对和 6 个基准测试中，TreeGraft 平均性能比两种固定单草稿策略中更优者提升 15.1%，最高增益达 26.6%。

Conclusion: TreeGraft 通过多草稿协同与智能调度机制，有效平衡了草稿生成速度与质量，显著优化了共享草稿树结构，从而大幅提升了大语言模型推测解码的整体效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through a draft-then-verify paradigm. Building on this, tree-structured methods improve inference by organizing proposals into multiple candidate paths, increasing the accepted length. However, existing tree-structured methods use a single drafter for all drafting steps, creating a dilemma: a smaller drafter is fast but yields lower-quality trees, whereas a larger drafter improves tree quality but suffers from high latency. To address this, we propose TreeGraft, a multi-drafter framework in which drafters of different costs jointly construct a shared draft tree. TreeGraft uses the stronger drafter to rescore candidates by updating scores assigned by the weaker drafter, reselect grafting positions, and recover promising paths left unexplored. It also integrates stronger drafter expansions non-destructively, preserving existing branches that may still be accepted by the target model. Together, these designs improve the quality of the shared draft tree. To control the drafting cost, TreeGraft introduces a lightweight scheduler distilled from an offline value system to decide when to call the stronger drafter. Across 10 model pairs and 6 benchmarks, TreeGraft outperforms the better of the two fixed single-drafter endpoint strategies by 15.1% on average, reaching a maximum gain of 26.6%. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TreeGraft-E983.

</details>


### [41] [ElementCheck: Complexity-Aware Long-Form Text Factuality Evaluation via Sentence Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26118)
*Xinming Wang,Haoran Du,Yi Chen,Jian Xu,Hongming Yang,Han Hu,Yulong Chen,Cheng-Lin Liu,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElementCheck 是一个复杂度感知的长文本事实性评估框架，通过句子元素图结构实现更精准、高效的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文本事实性评估方法依赖“分解-检索-验证”流程，但存在因主张分解引入噪声和验证粒度固定导致结果不可靠的问题。

Method: 提出 ElementCheck 框架：1) 从句子中提取通过可验证连接显式链接的实体对作为元素；2) 构建元素图以表征句子结构复杂度；3) 根据复杂度动态选择直接验证或元素级细化验证策略；4) 构建 FastFact-Sent 基准用于细粒度评估。

Result: 在 FastFact-Sent 及两个领域特定基准上，ElementCheck 在五个骨干模型上均提升了事实性验证性能，同时保持了良好的准确率-成本权衡；复杂度感知验证减少了不必要的重复验证并保持跨模型稳定性。

Conclusion: ElementCheck 通过元素图结构实现复杂度感知的事实性验证，有效克服了传统方法的噪声与粒度限制，为长文本事实性评估提供了更高效、稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing long-form factuality evaluation relies on the decompose-retrieve-verify pipeline. However, the pipeline suffers from noise from claim decomposition and fixed verification granularity, resulting in unreliable results. We propose ElementCheck, a complexity-aware framework that verifies long-form outputs via sentence elements. Instead of uniformly decomposing sentences into atomic sub-claims, ElementCheck extracts entity pairs that are explicitly linked through verifiable connections in the original sentence as elements, and organizes these into an element graph. The graph topology provides a structural signal for estimating sentence complexity, enabling direct verification for simple sentences and targeted element-level refinement and verification for complex ones. To support fine-grained evaluation, we construct a new benchmark FastFact-Sent by mapping isolated claims from FastFact-Bench back to their source sentences. Experiments on FastFact-Sent and two domain-specific benchmarks show ElementCheck consistently improves factuality verification across five backbone models while maintaining a favorable accuracy-cost trade-off. Further analyses demonstrate that complexity-aware verification reduces unnecessary re-verification and maintains stability across different backbones.

</details>


### [42] [DeflectBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Rhetorical Fallacy Generation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26119)
*Art Kanke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过DeflectBench基准测试评估了四个前沿大语言模型在诱导下生成修辞谬误（如人身攻击、红鲱鱼等）的能力，发现模型的拒绝行为主要取决于提示词的结构而非内容本身；特定的“教育辩论教练”提示框架可将拒绝率降至接近零，但模型通常会标记出其生成的操纵性内容，而非完全隐蔽地顺从。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注检测文本中已有的修辞谬误，而较少关注大语言模型是否会被提示按需生成谬误，以及当前的安全后训练是否有效约束了此类行为。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建了DeflectBench基准，评估了四个前沿模型生成的23,990个样本。实验涵盖了三种规避策略（whataboutism, ad hominem, red herring）、七种提示框架以及跨越四个争议级别的80个主张，以此分析模型的拒绝机制和合规行为。

Result: 1. 拒绝行为主要由请求结构而非主张内容决定：80个主张间的拒绝率差异仅11%，而提示框架或谬误类型的改变可导致拒绝率波动近100%或超过80%。
2. “教育辩论教练”提示框架可将所有模型家族的拒绝率降至接近零。
3. 模型在被绕过安全限制时，通常表现为“标记合规”（labeled compliance），即在生成操纵性内容的同时明确指出其为请求的操纵手段，而非“纯净合规”（clean compliance）。
4. 四个模型在拒绝、标记合规、软拒绝和纯净合规的分布上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型生成修辞谬误的行为高度依赖于提示词的构建方式，而非输入内容的争议性。虽然特定的提示框架可以绕过安全拒绝机制，但模型倾向于通过标记其输出来保持某种程度的透明度，这表明当前的安全后训练在防止显式拒绝的同时，仍保留了部分对操纵行为的自我揭示能力。

Abstract: Whether large language models can be prompted to generate rhetorical fallacies on demand, and whether current safety post-training constrains this behavior, has received less attention than the related question of detecting fallacies in existing text. We close this gap with DeflectBench, evaluating 23,990 generations from four frontier models across three deflection strategies (whataboutism, ad hominem, red herring), seven prompt framings, and 80 claims spanning four controversy levels. Refusal is governed primarily by request structure rather than claim content. Per claim refusal varies by only 11 percentage points across the 80 claims, while a single prompt frame change can swing within model refusal by nearly 100 percentage points and switching the requested fallacy type can swing it by over 80 percentage points within explicit framings. An educational debate coach prompt framing collapses refusal to near zero across all four model families, but the bypassed behavior is not clean compliance. Models typically produce labeled compliance, naming the requested manipulation in the same response that contains it. The four models distribute differently across refusal, labeled compliance, soft refusal, and clean compliance. The code and dataset are released at https://github.com/ArtKanke/DeflectBench.

</details>


### [43] [Recipes for Steering and Scaling LLMs via Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26120)
*Jiajun He,Zongyu Guo,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Yuanqi Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于采样（Sequential Monte Carlo 和 Replica Exchange）的理论框架，用于引导和扩展自回归大语言模型（LLM）的生成，无需外部监督或奖励模型即可提升生成质量，且效果优于 Best-of-N 和标准 MCMC 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型采样策略在研究超越基础模型的更丰富目标分布时，效率仍然低下，缺乏灵活且理论扎实的引导与扩展方法。

Method: 提出了一个灵活的、理论基础的框架，包含两种算法：基于序列蒙特卡洛（SMC）和基于副本交换（RE）。该框架通过幂次化、乘积或倾斜基础模型分布来引导生成过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在无需外部监督或奖励模型的情况下，能有效扩展 LLM 的生成质量，其扩展性优于 Best-of-N 和标准 MCMC 基线。

Conclusion: 该论文为通过采样进行大语言模型概率推理提供了一套系统化的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are probabilistic models, typically defined by an autoregressive factorization. While recent work has begun to study richer target distributions beyond the base model, the sampling strategies remain highly inefficient. In this paper, we present a flexible and theoretically grounded framework for steering and scaling autoregressive LLMs with sampling. Within this framework, we describe two algorithms -- one based on Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) and one based on Replica Exchange (RE) -- that steer generation toward powering, product or tilting of the base model distribution. We illustrate this framework through scaling the generation quality of LLMs without external supervision or reward models. Experimental results demonstrate our methods scale more favorably than Best-of-N and standard MCMC baselines. Overall, this paper offers a systematic recipe for probabilistic inference with LLMs via sampling.

</details>


### [44] [Can a Model Catch Its Own Hallucinations for Free?: Label-Free Doubt Signals Hold Their Own Against a Labelled Dataset for Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26121)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注数据的模型自我校准方法，利用模型自身置信度信号指导其在不确定时选择"不确定"而非猜测，实验表明该方法效果与有监督方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会同样流畅地陈述错误事实，但模型内部往往"知道"何时处于不确定状态（错误答案的置信度概率往往较低）。传统方法需要标注数据集来教模型何时放弃回答，本文探索是否可以利用模型自身的置信度信号（无需标注）来实现这一目标。

Method: 使用LoRA对六个开源权重模型（1B-8B规模，两个家族）进行微调，训练模型在高置信度时回答、低置信度时说"我不确定"，仅使用置信度信号而不使用正确答案标签，并通过独立裁判模型判定答案正确性。

Result: 在匹配的覆盖率下，该无标签方法与有监督的放弃调优方法之间没有统计上可检测的差异；控制实验表明增益来自校准而非死记硬背；但该信号无法识别"自信的错误事实"这一盲点。

Conclusion: 模型的自我怀疑可以作为标注数据集的近免费替代品，用于教导模型何时应该放弃回答，为减少模型幻觉提供了一种低成本解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models state false facts as fluently as true ones, yet a model often "knows" internally when it is on shaky ground: the probability it assigns to its own answer tends to dip on the facts it gets wrong. The usual way to act on this, teaching a model to abstain rather than guess, requires a labelled dataset of right and wrong answers. We ask whether the model's own confidence, which is free and needs no labels, can do that job instead. We fine-tune each model (with LoRA) to answer when its frozen confidence is high and to say "I'm not sure" when it is low, using the signal alone and no correctness labels. Across six open-weights models (1B-8B, two families) on short-form factual question answering, with correctness adjudicated by an independent judge model, this label-free recipe holds its own against label-supervised abstention-tuning: at matched coverage we find no statistically detectable difference between the two. A control that drills hard examples instead of abstaining does not help, indicating the gain comes from calibration, not rote memorization. The signal's one blind spot is confidently wrong facts, which it cannot flag. A model's own doubt is thus a near-free substitute for a labelled dataset when teaching it when to abstain. Code and artifacts are available on request.

</details>


### [45] [Which India Survives Translation? Narrative Homogenisation Across Indian Oral Traditions in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26123)
*Paarth Singh Rathore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算实验发现，大语言模型（LLM）在生成印度三种不同地区传统故事时存在部分同质化现象，且使用地区语言提示反而比英语提示降低了文化保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 主要在英语互联网文本上训练，可能过度代表某些文化叙事，引发对非西方叙事传统多样性被同质化的担忧。

Method: 收集三种印度传统（拉贾斯坦 Pabuji 史诗、泰米尔 Sangam 诗歌、孟加拉民间故事）的真实参考语料，使用 Claude Sonnet 和 Gemini 模型，通过三种提示类型（通用、文化特定、地区语言）进行 54 次生成请求，利用 Sentence-BERT 嵌入和余弦相似度测量参考漂移和跨传统收敛度。

Result: 输出虽更接近自身传统参考文本，但跨传统相似度较高（0.52-0.66），表明存在部分同质化；意外发现地区语言提示比英语提示降低了对真实传统的保真度，拉贾斯坦和孟加拉传统降幅达 27 个百分点。

Conclusion: 该试点研究作为轻量级、可扩展的补充，支持近期关于 LLM 生成故事中印度文化误表征的大规模人工标注研究，揭示了多语言提示在模拟狭窄、较少记录的口头传统时的局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained predominantly on English-language internet text that over-represents certain cultural narratives, raising concerns that models flatten the diversity of non-Western storytelling traditions into a single homogenized archetype. We present a pilot computational study examining this across three maximally distinct Indian regional oral and literary traditions: the Rajasthani Pabuji epic, classical Tamil Sangam poetry, and Bengali folk tales. We collected authentic reference corpora for each tradition (11, 21, and 10 passages respectively) and prompted two LLMs (Claude Sonnet and Gemini) with 54 generation requests spanning three prompt types per tradition - generic, culturally specific, and regional-language. Using Sentence-BERT embeddings and cosine similarity, we measure reference drift (how closely outputs track their own tradition's authentic texts relative to the other two) and cross-tradition convergence (how similar outputs are across traditions). We find that while outputs remain closer to their own tradition's reference than to others, cross-tradition similarity is high (0.52-0.66) relative to what the traditions' genuine distance would predict, indicating partial homogenisation. Unexpectedly, prompting in the regional language (Hindi, Tamil, or Bengali) consistently reduced fidelity to the authentic tradition relative to English prompting, by as much as 27 percentage points for Rajasthani and Bengali traditions. We discuss this against conflicting prior results on multilingual prompting and argue it reflects a difference between eliciting general cultural diversity and simulating one narrow, lesser-documented oral tradition. We position this pilot as a lightweight, scalable complement to recent large-scale human-annotation studies of Indian cultural misrepresentation in LLM-generated stories, as part of a broader doctoral research program.

</details>


### [46] [Compositional Generalization via Structural Identification in a Category-Theoretic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26465)
*Akihiro Maeda,Thomas Seiller,Yohei Oseki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖模型训练的组合泛化分析方法，通过结构/词汇识别判断COGS测试样本的可接受性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合泛化评估依赖模型准确率，缺乏对训练数据本身许可能力的诊断分析。

Method: 将句子表示为从语法地址到词汇标记的函子，通过选择性折叠诱导Kan扩张传播观测关联，分析21种COGS泛化类型的可接受性识别特征。

Result: 不同泛化类型呈现独特的识别特征，剩余失败案例可分离出未支持的结构模板。

Conclusion: 该方法能在不训练预测模型的情况下，表征训练语料在特定识别下所许可的内容，提供数据侧诊断。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is usually evaluated through model accuracy. We instead ask which structural or lexical identifications make held-out COGS examples admissible from the structures observed in training. Sentences are represented as functors from syntactic addresses to lexical tokens, and selective collapses induce Kan extensions that propagate observed associations. Across 21 COGS generalization types, admissibility follows distinct identification profiles, while residual failures separate unsupported structural templates. These data-side diagnoses characterize what the training corpus licenses under specified identifications, without training a predictive model.

</details>


### [47] [Natural-Language Policies to Executable Decisions: An Interpretable Large Language Model Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26124)
*Ziqiang Zhang,Jing Ma,Zilong Wang,Jiayuan Chen,Yi Qiao,Yu He,Wei Zhang,Dai Cheng,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生产级的大语言模型（LLM）驱动定价系统，通过严格界定LLM的决策边界（仅负责结构化提取和策略路径选择），结合确定性数值计算和可解释的条件树，解决了大规模旅游场景中非结构化订单与复杂定价策略的自动化难题，显著提升了效率并降低了人力成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模旅游定价自动化中的挑战：旅游订单高度非结构化，而定价策略复杂、快速演变且开放。传统规则引擎脆弱且维护成本高，而无约束的LLM代理缺乏金融决策所需的可靠性和可审计性。

Method: 构建混合架构系统：1. 严格限制LLM决策边界，仅执行结构化提取和有界策略/路径选择；2. 所有数值定价（含总价计算）由确定性逻辑执行；3. 将策略编译为可解释的条件树，支持无需代码更改即可适应新条款和规则演变，并提供可审计的人工介入接口；4. 基于日志轨迹进行周期性微调以优化树诱导和路径匹配。

Result: 系统在一家市级国有旅游企业部署，覆盖7个景区、12个业务类别、1500+运营商及1000+活跃策略。六个月内处理3960个订单，订单管理团队从15-20人缩减至3人，单笔订单处理时间从10分钟降至2分钟以内。

Conclusion: 该系统成功平衡了LLM的灵活性与金融系统的可靠性，通过“LLM提取+确定性计算+可解释策略树”的架构，实现了高可扩展性、可审计性及显著的效率提升，为复杂动态环境下的自动化决策提供了可行的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Pricing automation in large-scale tourism is challenging because travel orders are highly unstructured, while pricing policies are complex, rapidly evolving, and inherently open-ended. Traditional rule engines are brittle and costly to maintain, whereas unconstrained LLM agents lack the reliability and auditability required for financial decisions. We present a production-grade LLM-powered pricing system with a strict decision boundary: LLMs perform structured extraction and bounded policy/path selection, while all numeric pricing, including total-price computation, is executed deterministically. Policies are compiled into interpretable condition trees, enabling open-ended support for new clauses and evolving rules without code changes, while exposing auditable artifacts for human-in-the-loop control. Periodic fine-tuning on logged traces further improves tree induction and path matching. Deployed at a municipal state-owned tourism enterprise across 7 scenic sites and 12 business categories with 1,500+ operators and 1,000+ active policies, the system processed 3,960 orders in six months, reduced the order management team from 15-20 to 3, and cut per-order handling time from 10 minutes to <2 minutes.

</details>


### [48] [Training-Time Explainability for Multilingual Hate Speech Detection: Aligning Model Reasoning with Human Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26125)
*Muhammad Deedahwar Mazhar Qureshi,Sannaan Khan,Muhammad Atif Qureshi,Wael Rashwan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种训练时可解释性框架，通过将模型推理与人工标注的理由对齐，解决了针对穆斯林社区的隐性、多语言仇恨言论检测难题，在提升分类性能的同时增强了模型的可解释性和文化感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对穆斯林社区的在线仇恨言论常以文化编码和多语言形式出现，逃避传统AI审核；现有系统虽准确但缺乏透明度，易产生偏见、过度审查或审查不足，且往往脱离社会文化语境。

Method: 提出一种训练时可解释性框架，利用LIME、集成梯度（Integrated Gradients）、Grad X Input和注意力机制（attention）进行评估，通过基于梯度和注意力的正则化方法，将模型推理与人工标注理由对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法为构建多语言、具有文化感知能力的内容审核系统提供了可行路径，有效平衡了检测性能与模型可解释性。

Abstract: Online hate against Muslim communities often appears in culturally coded, multilingual forms that evade conventional AI moderation. Such systems, though accurate, remain opaque and risk bias, over-censorship, or under-moderation, particularly when detached from sociocultural context. We propose a \emph{training-time} explainability framework that aligns model reasoning with human-annotated rationales, improving both classification performance and interpretability. Our approach is evaluated on HateXplain (English) and BullySent (Hinglish), reflecting the prevalence of anti-Muslim hate across both languages. Using LIME, Integrated Gradients, Grad X Input, and attention, we assess accuracy, explanation quality, and cross-method agreement. Results show that gradient- and attention-based regularization improve F-scores, enhance plausibility and faithfulness, and capture culturally specific cues for detecting implicit anti-Muslim hate, offering a path toward multilingual, culturally aware content moderation.

</details>

*Bohao Wang,Chenwei Wu,Haoyu Li,Hang Zou,Yu Tian,Lina Bariah,Li Wei,Chongwen Huang,Yongliang Shen,Zhaoyang Zhang,Merouane Debbah*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前电信领域大语言模型集成中的双向能力差距：通用推理模型缺乏电信特定领域的 grounding（如规范、遥测、故障证据及射频计算），而专用电信模型则在结构化多步推理能力上存在局限。

Method: 1. 数据构建： curated 67,427条样本的监督微调（SFT）语料库，涵盖协议、知识、建模、故障四个推理轴，结合特定轴的思维链（CoT）生成与前缀延续自验证。
2. 模型训练：基于Qwen3.5-9B，采用两阶段后训练策略。第一阶段：利用多教师低秩适应（LoRA）进行SFT，注入电信知识并诱导特定轴推理格式。第二阶段：使用群相对策略优化（GRPO），通过解耦裁剪和动态采样策略优化（DAPO）稳定训练，并利用四个轴对齐的二元验证器奖励优化策略。

Result: TelecomGPT-R1-9B在GSMA开放电信排行榜中位居榜首；在七个公共电信基准测试中，其表现优于其他开源电信大语言模型，且七轴平均性能可与最先进的闭源前沿推理模型相媲美。

Conclusion: TelecomGPT-R1-9B成功桥接了通用推理与领域专用性之间的差距，证明了通过结构化多轴语料构建与结合LoRA及改进型强化学习（GRPO+DAPO）的两阶段训练策略，能够有效提升大模型在复杂电信工程工作流中的推理与 grounding 能力。

Abstract: Telecommunications is a high-leverage domain for large language model (LLM)-based reasoning because routine engineering workflows require joint grounding in normative specifications, operational telemetry, vendor-specific fault evidence, and exact RF/network calculations. However, current LLM integration in telecom remains bottlenecked by a two-sided capability gap: generic reasoners often lack telecom-specific grounding, while domain-specific telecom LLMs remain limited in structured, multi-step reasoning. To bridge this gap, we release TelecomGPT-R1-9B, a unified open-source telecom reasoner that ranks top-performing on the GSMA open telco leaderboard. Specifically, we curate a 67,427-example supervised fine-tuning (SFT) corpus organized around four complementary reasoning axes: protocol, knowledge, modeling, and fault. The corpus is built from axis-matched public web sources and enhanced through axis-specific chain-of-thought (CoT) generation and prefix-continuation self-validation. Starting from Qwen3.5-9B, we further develop a two-stage post-training recipe. First, multi-teacher low-rank adaptation (LoRA)-based SFT injects telecom knowledge and induces axis-specific reasoning formats. Second, group relative policy optimization (GRPO), stabilized by decoupled clip and dynamic sampling policy optimization (DAPO), optimizes the policy using four axis-aligned binary verifier rewards. Across seven public telecom benchmarks, TelecomGPT-R1-9B ranks first among open-source telecom LLMs and achieves a seven-axis mean comparable to state-of-the-art closed-source frontier reasoners.

</details>


### [50] [FIRSTPASS: A Multi-Domain, Multi-Round Peer Review Dataset Grounded in Real Editorial Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26129)
*Prabhjot Singh,Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FIRSTPASS，这是首个基于多学科高影响力期刊（Nature Communications）完整多轮编辑对话的大规模同行评审数据集，涵盖生物学、化学等五个领域，填补了现有AI评审模型仅局限于计算机领域的空白，并提供了真实的编辑决策结果标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学同行评审数据集仅训练于计算机科学和机器学习领域，导致AI系统缺乏对生物学、化学等其他学科特定评审标准（如污染控制、NMR谱图分配）的理解，无法跨学科进行有效的科学验证评估。

Method: 从Nature Communications自2022年11月起实施的强制透明同行评审制度中策划数据，构建包含3,668条记录的数据集，覆盖五个科学领域；数据结构包含初审报告、作者逐点回复及更新后的评审评估，并根据编辑决策（两轮为标准，三轮及以上为扩展）标注结果标签，同时通过自动化审计确保内容完整性。

Result: 构建了跨五个科学领域的3,668条完整多轮评审记录，专家评审平均字数达2,155字，密度显著高于会议评审；数据集包含基于真实编辑决策的基准标签，并通过自动化审计确认了100%的内容完整性。

Conclusion: FIRSTPASS数据集及其解析管道和评估脚本的发布，为跨学科AI科学判断的可复现基准测试提供了基础，使得AI系统能够学习并适应不同科学领域的特定评审逻辑和验证结构。

Abstract: Scientific peer review datasets have trained AI systems exclusively on Computer Science and Machine Learning venues, producing models that critique ablation studies yet have never seen a biology reviewer demand contamination controls or a chemist question Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR) spectral assignments. We introduce FIRSTPASS, the first large-scale peer review dataset built on complete multi-round editorial dialogues from a multidisciplinary high-impact journal. Curated from Nature Communications mandatory transparent peer review (instituted November 2022), FIRSTPASS comprises 3,668 records spanning five scientific domains (biology, chemistry, neuroscience, physics, and earth science), capturing the full iterative structure of scientific validation: initial referee reports, author point-by-point responses, and updated reviewer assessments. Each record carries an outcome label derived directly from editorial decisions (STANDARD for two-round review; EXTENDED for three or more rounds), providing ground truth absent in all prior corpora. An automated audit confirms 100% content integrity. Expert reviews average 2,155 words, substantially denser than conference venue reviews. All data, parsing pipelines, and evaluation scripts are released to enable reproducible benchmarking of AI scientific judgment across disciplines.

</details>


### [51] [Agents Don't Paginate: First-Chunk Selection for LLM Tool Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26130)
*Tatiana Petrova,Andrei Mazniak,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了编码智能体在处理超出 token 预算的工具响应时，是否应将关键信息（gold item）优先放在第一个分块（first chunk）中。实验表明，提高第一个分块的精度（p₁）并不会系统性提升下游准确率，因为智能体能从分块的任何位置恢复关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的编码智能体经常接收超出每轮 token 预算的工具响应。虽然协议支持分页，但观察发现智能体很少请求第二个分块。因此，研究关注关键信息在第一个分块中的位置（precision-at-1, p₁）是否影响智能体性能。

Method: 在受控离线基准测试中，将第一个分块选择视为 0/1 背包问题，在 500 个 SWE-bench Verified 任务上比较六种价值函数。随后通过单轮文件定位探测在五语言模型上测试 p₁ 的影响（4,800 次 LLM 调用）。

Result: 1. 提高 p₁ 不会系统性提升下游准确率，各模型差异小于 3 个百分点，无显著性。2. 添加四个文件元数据信号到关键词评分器会使 p₁ 降低 4.8 个百分点（p=0.001）。3. 无参数关键词评分器可将 p₁ 从 24.2% 提升至 35.0%（+10.8 p.p.），但下游准确率未变化。

Conclusion: 关键信息是否包含在第一个分块中比其在分块内的排名更重要。智能体能够从分块的任何位置恢复所需信息，因此优化第一个分块内的排名（rank-1）对最终性能无显著影响。

Abstract: Coding agents built on large language models (LLMs), such as Claude Code, Cursor, OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot, and Aider, receive tool responses that routinely exceed the agent's per-turn token budget. The standard remedy, pagination, is available in every protocol that produced these responses; yet across the corpus of session logs from a public Model Context Protocol middleware we observed no agent-initiated requests for a second chunk. The first chunk is what the agent reads, so we ask how often the gold item (the one the agent needs) is placed first in it: the precision-at-1 rate $p_1$.
  In a controlled offline benchmark we treat first-chunk selection as a 0/1 knapsack and compare six value functions on 500 SWE-bench Verified tasks, then test whether $p_1$ matters with a single-turn file-localisation probe on five language models (4,800 LLM calls; not an end-to-end resolve-rate test). Two pre-registered hypotheses did not hold and are our main findings. The central one is negative: raising $p_1$ does not systematically raise downstream accuracy. Per-model deltas stay under three percentage points (p.p.), are not consistently signed, and no model is significant; the agent recovers the gold from anywhere in the chunk, so what reaches its answer is first-chunk inclusion, not the gold's rank within it. The second: adding four file-metadata signals to a keyword scorer hurts $p_1$ by 4.8 p.p. (paired significance test, $p = 0.001$).
  A parameter-free keyword scorer does raise $p_1$, from a 24.2% baseline to 35.0% (+10.8 p.p., far beyond chance; $p = 3.9 \times 10^{-8}$), and to 35.8% with a fallback to the tool's native ordering when no keyword matches. But by our central finding this is a rank-1 gain, and rank-1 is the part that does not reach the agent's answer: downstream accuracy does not move.

</details>


### [52] [Which Metrics Save the Most Human Annotation? Prediction-Powered Evaluation and Meta-Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26638)
*Mingqi Gao,Anthony Sicilia,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“预测驱动评估”（Prediction-Powered Evaluation）框架，结合少量人工评估与大规模自动评分，实现高效且无偏的系统比较，并引入PPSR指标量化自动指标节省人工标注的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非可验证任务中人工评估可靠但昂贵、自动指标可扩展但有偏的矛盾，旨在降低人工标注成本而非完全取代人工判断。

Method: 基于预测驱动推断（PPI）构建评估框架，开发参数与非参数过程，分析配对与非配对设计的效率权衡，并在六个WMT数据集上验证；提出预测驱动节省率（PPSR）作为衡量自动指标实用性的元指标。

Result: 框架在六个WMT数据集上验证有效；PPSR相比现有系统级元指标能提供更具有区分力和稳定性的指标排名。

Conclusion: 该范式将自动指标重新定义为降低人工标注成本的工具，适用于广泛的非可验证任务，实现了数据高效且无偏的系统评估。

Abstract: Across various non-verifiable tasks, human evaluation is reliable but expensive, while automatic metrics are more scalable but often biased. Building on prediction-powered inference (PPI), we propose prediction-powered evaluation, a framework that combines limited human judgments with large-scale automatic scores to obtain data-efficient system comparisons that are provably unbiased. We develop parametric and non-parametric procedures, analyze the efficiency trade-off between paired and unpaired designs, and validate the framework on six WMT datasets. We further introduce the Prediction-Powered Saving Ratio (PPSR), a meta-metric that measures how much human annotation an automatic metric can save when used within prediction-powered evaluation. PPSR directly targets metric utility for prediction-powered evaluation and yields more discriminative and stable metric rankings than existing system-level meta-metrics. Overall, our new paradigm reframes automatic metrics as tools for reducing human annotation cost rather than replacing human judgment, and applies broadly to non-verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Evaluating Language Models in Realistic Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26131)
*Ilija Subasic,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了UPHELD基准，这是一个包含数千轮人类专家对话的大规模数据集，用于评估大语言模型在长对话中的一致性。研究发现现有自动指标和LLM裁判方法与人类判断相关性低，而提出的“混合裁判”框架将相关性提高了约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）被部署于开放域多轮交互，评估人类规模对话质量成为核心挑战。现有的针对摘要、翻译或短问答的评估框架无法充分衡量长对话的一致性，且其指标推导往往依赖合成数据而非真实人类来源，存在显著差距。

Method: 引入UPHELD基准，包含数百个由专业编剧创作的人类对人类完整对话，具有真实的回合密度，涵盖30,000+专家生成的对话回合及36,000+轮次人类标注。基于该基准，系统评估了经典自动指标和无参考的“LLM作为裁判”方法，并开发了结合多种评估信号的“混合裁判”（Mixture-of-Judges）框架。

Result: 实验表明，经典的自动指标和参考免费的LLM裁判方法在与专家人类判断的相关性上表现不可靠。相比之下，新开发的混合裁判框架通过与人类评估的相关性提高了约30%，显著提升了评估的准确性。

Conclusion: UPHELD填补了现有LLM数据集景观中的关键空白，为评估人类规模的对话智能提供了坚实、基于人类真实数据的基础，证明了结合多种评估信号的混合框架在提升评估可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to serve open-ended, multi-turn interactions, evaluating conversational quality at human scale has become a central challenge. Existing evaluation frameworks built for summarization, translation, or short-form QA tasks fall short of adequately measuring the consistency of human-scale dialogue, especially when derivation and validation of these metrics themselves often rely on synthetic rather than human sources. We fill the gap by introducing UPHELD (UPwork Human-Scale Evaluated Long Dialogues), a large, reference-full benchmark for evaluating human-scale conversational ability beyond factual correctness. UPHELD consists of hundreds of complete human-to-human dialogues authored by professional script writers, with realistic turn densities and 36,000+ per-turn human annotations across 30,000+ expert-generated dialogue turns. Using UPHELD, we systematically evaluate classical automatic metrics and reference-free LLM-as-a-judge approaches, and find them unreliable when correlated with expert human judgment. Building off this analysis, we use UPHELD to develop a Mixture-of-Judges framework that combines multiple evaluative signals and improves correlation with human assessments by approximately 30%. Overall, UPHELD provides a robust, human-grounded foundation for evaluating human-scale conversational intelligence that fills a crucial gap in the pre-existing LLM dataset landscape.

</details>


### [54] [Agent Seer: Synthesizing Scenarios from Specification Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26133)
*Harish Karumuri,Mahesh Vemula,David Lopes Pegna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Seer，一种仅利用工具规范（MCP）中的潜在语义信息，无需人工标注或实时工具访问，即可自动生成高质量、多轮对话评估场景的流水线，有效解决了现有基准构建成本高、扩展性差及静态化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估AI代理使用外部工具的方法依赖人工构建场景，这需要深厚的领域专业知识，难以在不同工具生态中扩展，且生成的基准是静态的，无法跟踪不断演变的API。

Method: 提出Agent Seer流水线，直接从单个模型上下文协议（MCP）规范出发，利用函数名、自然语言描述和类型化参数模式中的语义信息。该方法通过丰富原始模式、生成带有合成工具输出的分级场景，并将其扩展为基于模拟数据的多轮对话，无需示例、实时工具访问或特定领域微调。

Result: 在七个不同领域和工具规模的MCP规范上评估显示，该流水线在所有领域均表现出高质量，并在中小规模规范上实现了完整的工具覆盖率。分析发现，参数模式的复杂度是质量变化的最强相关因素，而工具集规模影响较小；参数值准确性是主要失败模式，这是粗粒度名称匹配指标无法察觉的。

Conclusion: 工具规范中编码的语义信息足以合成逼真的评估场景，Agent Seer证明了无需人工干预即可构建动态、可扩展且高保真的AI代理评估基准，其中参数值的准确性是提升评估质量的关键挑战。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents that use external tools requires realistic test scenarios that capture how practitioners compose tools and iterate across conversation turns. Constructing such scenarios by hand demands deep domain expertise, does not scale across tool ecosystems, and produces static benchmarks that cannot track evolving APIs. We observe that tool specifications -- function names, natural-language descriptions, and typed parameter schemas -- already encode sufficient semantic information to synthesize realistic evaluation scenarios without manual curation or live tool execution. Agent Seer builds off this latent information: from a single Model Context Protocol (MCP) specification, with no examples, no live tool access, and no domain-specific tuning. This pipeline enriches raw schemas, generates graded scenarios with synthetic tool outputs, and expands them into mock-data-grounded multi-turn dialogues that exhibit strong tool-calling correctness and conversational coherence.
  Evaluation quality is measured by applying this pipeline on seven MCP specifications spanning diverse domains and tool-suite sizes and measuring the tool-calling correctness and conversational coherence. The pipeline achieves strong quality across all domains, with complete tool coverage on small and medium specifications. Two findings emerge within this analysis: parameter schema complexity is the strongest correlate of quality variation -- tool-suite size plays a smaller, orthogonal role -- and argument value accuracy is the dominant failure mode among imperfect scenarios, a sub-dimension invisible to coarse-grained name-match metrics.

</details>


### [55] [Data Science Approaches to Evaluating Honours Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26135)
*Francesca von Braun-Bates,Sunreeta Sen,Indraayudh Talukdar,Anirban Lahiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化数据科学流程，利用开源情报（OSINT）和自然语言处理（NLP）技术，从碎片化网络数据中评估公众对个人的情感倾向，并验证了专用算法MINOS在识别声誉风险方面优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从异构、非结构化的开源情报中提取可审计的个人层面情感分布的难题，旨在为高风险决策（如荣誉授予）提供透明、可复现且有人类参与的情感评估支持。

Method: 构建包含网络搜索、文本提取、相关性过滤、分词、共指消解和情感分析的链式NLP流水线；对比传统算法（AFINN, VADER）与领域专用算法MINOS（针对声誉风险、不当行为及公共贡献设计）在已知声誉结果的公众人物数据上的表现。

Result: 在应用于英国荣誉体系案例时，MINOS算法在区分正面、模糊和负面案例方面表现出最清晰的分离度，优于AFINN和VADER。

Conclusion: 链式NLP与OSINT方法能够有效支持高风险决策中透明、可复现的人机协同情感评估，特别是在需要维持高公共行为标准的场景中。

Abstract: We present a modular data-science pipeline for estimating public sentiment towards individuals from fragmented, unstructured open-source intelligence (OSINT). The method chains web search, text extraction, relevance filtering, tokenisation, co-reference resolution, and sentiment analysis to convert heterogeneous web material into auditable person-level sentiment distributions. We compare AFINN and VADER with MINOS, a domain-informed sentiment algorithm designed to detect language associated with reputational risk, misconduct, and positive public contribution. Applied to public figures with known reputational outcomes, MINOS gives the clearest separation between positive, ambiguous, and negative cases. The results show that chained NLP and OSINT methods can support transparent, reproducible, human-in-the-loop sentiment assessment for high-stakes decision support. We demonstrate the approach on the UK Honours system, where individuals are required to display high standards of public conduct to maintain an Honour.

</details>


### [56] [Reward-Informed Sparse Autoencoders and the Solution-Completeness Confound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26136)
*Tanvi Nagilla,Alexander Jameson,Daniel Manta,Shayaan Uddin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出奖励感知稀疏自编码器（RI-SAE），利用强化学习产生的奖励信号筛选数据训练SAE，发现其分离“好/坏”推理轨迹的能力主要源于答案完整性（如是否包含框选答案）等表面形式特征，而非深层推理质量；通用SAE无法复现该分离效果，表明这是特定信号的内拟合，仅少量特征涉及符号数学或程序性语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将强化学习产生的奖励信号有效应用于稀疏自编码器（SAE），以提取语言模型中可解释的推理特征，从而提升对模型推理机制的理解。

Method: 构建奖励感知SAE（RI-SAE）：将GRPO轨迹按奖励高低分为“好/坏”两类，在Llama-3.1-8B模型激活值上训练标准JumpReLU SAE；通过轮廓系数评估特征分离度，并设计控制实验（TF-IDF文本分类器、结构线索分析、通用SAE对比）以区分分离效果是源于推理质量还是答案形式。

Result: RI-SAE在筛选出的稀疏特征子集上实现高轮廓系数（0.79），但控制实验表明该分离主要由答案完整性驱动（如99%“好”轨迹含框选答案 vs 69%“坏”轨迹）；TF-IDF分类器AUC达0.75–0.83，仅三个结构线索（长度、闭合推理块、框选答案）即达AUC 0.70；通用SAE完全无法分离类别（轮廓系数0.01），证实高分离度是奖励信号的内拟合而非普适推理结构。

Conclusion: 奖励过滤是一种低成本、无需额外标注的复用RL信号方法，但其提取的特征多反映答案完成形式而非推理质量；仅少数可读特征（符号数学、程序性与评估性语言）具启发性，需结合控制实验谨慎解读SAE结果。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language-model activations into sparse, interpretable features, and an appealing way to aim them at reasoning is to curate their data with a signal reinforcement learning already produces: the reward. We build such a reward-informed SAE (RI-SAE): we split GRPO trajectories into high-reward ("good") and low-reward ("bad") reasoning continuations, train a standard JumpReLU SAE on their activations, and then ask what the resulting good/bad separation actually measures. On Llama-3.1-8B a sparse subset of the 16,384 features does separate the classes (silhouette 0.79 on the selected features versus 0.005 for the full code), but a control battery shows the separation is largely solution completeness rather than reasoning quality: a TF-IDF text classifier already splits the classes (AUC 0.75--0.83), and three structural cues alone (length, a closed reasoning block, and a boxed answer) reach AUC 0.70 (99% of good versus 69% of bad completions are boxed). A generic SAE that never saw the reward does not separate the classes at all (silhouette 0.01, no discriminative features), so the 0.79 is in-sample fitting of this curated signal rather than structure that a reward-blind dictionary recovers. We therefore present the recipe and its control battery together: reward filtering is a cheap, label-free way to reuse RL signals for interpretability, but most of what it surfaces is completion form. Two discriminative features are still readable (symbolic mathematics; procedural and evaluative language), which we take as illustrative rather than as isolated reasoning.

</details>


### [57] [Interpretable, Fairly Evaluated Automated L2 Speaking Assessment that Beats the Single-Human Ceiling and Why Pause Encoding Does Not Change LLM Fluency Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26137)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合可解释特征与大语言模型（LLM）的混合系统，用于评估第二语言（L2）英语口语，在无需拟合人类标签的情况下，其评分与人类共识的相关性（rho=0.818）优于81%的个体评分员，且通过多项控制实验验证了特征的有效性与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二语言学习者缺乏口语练习伙伴，且口语技能焦虑感强，导致自动化口语练习与评分市场需求增长，但自动化评分需具备准确性、可解释性、公平性并与人类标准对齐才能值得信赖。

Method: 构建可解释特征与LLM混合模型，使用De-Jong语音时序复合作为确定性特征，结合单个文本LLM流利度判断，在ICNALE全球评分档案（140篇演讲，约80名训练评分员，10项分析标准）上进行评估，采用学习者隔离方法、配对bootstrap置信区间、独白负对照、特征复现及L1公平性审计进行验证。

Result: 确定性语音时序复合作达到rho=0.764，与LLM流利度判断融合后Spearman rho达0.818，优于81%的个体评分员（中位数rho=0.73），接近最佳评分员，达到可靠性校正最大值的83%；混合模型比单独复合提升+0.054（95% CI [0.017, 0.108]）；暂停编码的对照实验显示效果边界在+/-0.1 rho内，流利度信号主要来自测量的语音时序特征而非LLM提示中的暂停写法。

Conclusion: 该混合系统在不拟合人类标签的情况下实现了高准确性与可解释性的L2口语评分，验证了语音时序特征的核心作用，并通过严格实验设计确保了结果的可靠性与公平性，为自动化口语评分提供了可信解决方案。

Abstract: Second-language (L2) English learners can rarely rehearse speaking with a partner. Speaking is also the most anxiety-laden skill. These gaps drive a fast-growing market for automated speaking practice and scoring. But an automated score is trustworthy only if it is accurate, interpretable, fair, and benchmarked against the right human bar. We build an interpretable feature-plus-LLM hybrid for spontaneous L2 dialogue. We evaluate it without ever fitting to the human labels, against the ICNALE Global Rating Archive: 140 speeches rated by ~80 trained raters on 10 analytic criteria. We score the 130 L2 speeches with usable audio. A deterministic De-Jong speech-timing composite reaches rho=0.764. Blended with a single text-LLM fluency judgment, it reaches Spearman rho=0.818 against the consensus gold. This agrees with the consensus better than 81% of the 80 individual trained raters: above the median rater (rho=0.73) and near the best, and at ~83% of the reliability-corrected maximum (kappa_max=0.99). The blend improves on the composite alone by +0.054 (paired-bootstrap 95% CI [0.017, 0.108], excludes 0); the LLM adds a coarse fluency ranking that the continuous composite refines. We also report a controlled null on pause encoding, bounded to effects below about +/-0.1 rho at this sample size. Holding the LLM and learner words fixed and varying only how pauses are written into the prompt, inline pause locations do not beat aggregate pause statistics (-0.069, CI [-0.15, +0.08]), and a grounded mid-clause criterion gives no reliable gain. The fluency signal comes from the measured speech-timing features, not from how pauses are written for the LLM. We back every claim with two agreeing learner-isolation methods, paired-bootstrap CIs, a monologue negative control, per-feature reproduction of classical measurements, and a per-L1 fairness audit.

</details>


### [58] [Cross-Platform Generalisation Failure in Mental Health Natural Language Processing: A Five-Axis Fairness Audit of Transformer Models on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26138)
*Rajveer Singh Pall,Sameer Yadav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CPFE框架，评估四个Transformer模型在跨平台心理健康数据上的表现，发现跨平台性能显著下降，需将跨平台验证作为心理健康NLP系统的标准要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康NLP系统在不同平台（如Reddit、Twitter）上的泛化能力和公平性尚未得到系统评估，需要建立标准化的跨平台评估框架以确保模型在异构环境中的可靠性。

Method: 提出五轴CPFE评估框架（判别性能、校准、统计显著性、预测公平性、归因稳定性），在Kaggle数据集上训练四个Transformer模型，并在Reddit和Twitter测试集上进行跨平台评估。

Result: 跨平台AUC显著下降（Reddit下降30.3-35.4%，Twitter下降37.9-39.5%），校准误差大幅上升（ECE从0.056-0.060升至0.196-0.542），预测公平性差异显著，词汇归因几乎完全 divergent。

Conclusion: 跨平台验证应成为心理健康NLP系统的标准要求，目标平台标签作为训练信号比校准信号更有价值。

Abstract: We introduce the Cross-Platform Fairness Evaluation (CPFE) framework -- a five-axis audit protocol covering discriminative performance, calibration, statistical significance, prediction equity, and attribution stability -- and apply it to four transformer models (BERT, RoBERTa, Emotion-DistilRoBERTa, GoEmotions-RoBERTa) trained on a Kaggle mental health corpus (n=35,556) and evaluated on Reddit (n=6,257) and Twitter (n=2,883) test sets with emotion labels mapped to clinical proxies. All three independently evaluated models exhibit consistent and substantial cross-platform AUC degradation (30.3-35.4% on Reddit, 37.9-39.5% on Twitter) relative to within-platform performance (AUC 0.983-0.987), confirmed across five independent training seeds. Calibration failure is concurrent and severe: ECE rises from 0.056-0.060 in-domain to 0.196-0.229 on Reddit and 0.499-0.542 on Twitter. Platform-specific temperature scaling reduces mean ECE by 88.0% without altering discriminative performance (mean |delta AUC|<0.01), confirming separable failure modes. Prediction equity analysis reveals large cross-platform disparities (raw DI < 0.17; prior-shift-adjusted DI: 0.11-0.29 on Reddit), with equalized odds differences of 0.753-0.830 for mental health proxy classes on Reddit and 0.755-0.831 for anxiety on Twitter. Attribution stability analysis shows near-complete vocabulary divergence across platforms (Jaccard J=0 in 14/16 model-class pairs at K=10). These findings support treating cross-platform validation across all five CPFE axes as a standard requirement for mental health NLP systems in heterogeneous environments. In a single-seed fine-tuning experiment, mean AUC improved by 0.216, suggesting target-platform labels provide greater benefit as training signal than as calibration signal.

</details>


### [59] [Syntax vs. Semantics: How Transformers Learn Deep Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26139)
*Jiangrui Zhao,Xiaoting Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机制框架，解释大语言模型在学习深层语义依赖时出现的"梯度饥饿"现象，并基于此提出了拓扑对齐对比目标，显著提升了变量绑定任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然表现出卓越的句法流畅性，但其获取深层语义依赖的优化动力学仍不清楚，特别是为何结构推理能力会突然涌现。

Method: 1. 提出表面统计与深层语义竞争的机制框架；2. 理论分析识别"梯度饥饿"现象；3. 验证Chain-of-Thought策略的有效性机制；4. 提出拓扑对齐对比目标优化方法。

Result: 1. 发现稀疏语义依赖的误差信号在早期优化中被抑制；2. 解释了结构推理能力突然涌现的相变现象；3. 在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-Coder-7B等模型上验证了发现；4. 新方法在变量绑定任务上比标准交叉熵微调提升超过2倍。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了大语言模型语义学习的优化动力学机制，为理解CoT策略提供了理论基础，并提出了有效的改进方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models demonstrate remarkable syntactic fluency, yet the optimization dynamics governing their acquisition of deep semantic dependencies remain poorly understood. We propose a mechanistic framework that models this learning process as a competition between Surface Statistics and Deep Semantics. Our theoretical analysis identifies a ``Gradient Starvation" phenomenon where the error signals for sparse semantic dependencies are actively suppressed during early optimization. This suppression impedes the learning of structural reasoning and causes its emergence to manifest as a sudden phase transition. Furthermore, this framework offers a mechanistic basis for the effectiveness of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) strategies. By externalizing intermediate reasoning steps into concrete tokens, CoT effectively bypasses the suppression regime inherent to implicit reasoning. We validate these findings across scales ranging from toy transformers to production models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B). Finally, guided by this theory, we propose a topology-aligned contrastive objective that explicitly rectifies the gradient geometry. Experiments on variable binding tasks demonstrate that our method achieves an improvement that is over 2x larger than that obtained via standard cross-entropy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [60] [Affix Cache for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26140)
*Kaihua Liang,An Zhong,Xin Tan,Zafar Ayyub Qazi,Hong Xu,Jian Weng,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ACache，一种针对扩散大语言模型（DLLM）的基于词缀的缓存复用机制，通过识别并仅重计算少量关键的“锚点词缀”，在保持精度的同时显著降低了推理延迟并提升了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（DLLM）虽支持非自回归解码和双向上下文建模，但其高效推理面临挑战。由于双向注意力机制将共享上下文的关键值（KV）状态与不断生成的token耦合，导致传统的基于前缀的KV缓存复用失效（数据过时），而全量重计算则开销巨大。

Method: 提出ACache机制，通过测量词缀token对被掩码生成token的影响，识别出请求特定的关键子集（称为“锚点token”）。该机制仅选择性地重计算这些锚点token的KV状态，而复用其余的词缀缓存。该方案构建于Fast-dLLM和Nano-vLLM引擎之上。

Result: 在重计算约20%的词缀token时，ACache在不同设置下恢复了直接复用词缀缓存导致的精度损失。实验显示，该方法将重计算延迟降低了高达55.7%，并将端到端吞吐量提升了1.68倍。

Conclusion: ACache有效解决了DLLM中因双向注意力导致的缓存复用难题，通过稀疏化的关键token重计算策略，实现了推理效率与模型精度之间的最佳平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) enable non-autoregressive decoding and bidirectional context modeling, but efficient inference remains challenging. Unlike autoregressive systems, whose key-value (KV) cache can be reused for shared prefixes, DLLMs couple the KV states of shared context tokens with evolving generated tokens through bidirectional attention, making naive cache reuse stale while full recomputation is expensive. We present ACache, an affix-oriented cache reuse mechanism for shared text spans in DLLMs beyond prefixes. ACache identifies a small request-specific subset of critical affix tokens, called Anchor Tokens, by measuring their influence on masked generation tokens, and selectively recomputes the KV states of only these tokens while reusing the remaining affix cache. Built on Fast-dLLM, ACache recovers the accuracy loss caused by direct affix-cache reuse across different settings when recomputing around 20% of affix tokens. We also build a shared-prefix prototype on top of the Nano-vLLM engine, showing that ACache reduces recompute latency by up to 55.7% and improves end-to-end throughput by up to 1.68$\times$.

</details>


### [61] [AdaThinking-E: One-Token Entropy Regulation for Adaptive Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26141)
*Zining Wang,Tongkun Guan,Boming Chen,Zhentao Guo,Jianqiang Liu,Chao Jin,Chen Duan,Kai Zhou,Pengfei Yan,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaThinking-E框架，通过单token熵调节实现自适应思考，使多模态大模型能根据问题复杂度智能决定是否启用推理，在保持复杂任务准确性的同时提升简单任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型对所有问题统一应用深度推理，导致简单任务产生不必要的计算开销，影响用户体验和基准数据集准确性，需要自适应思考机制根据问题复杂度智能决定是否启用推理。

Method: 提出AdaThinking-E强化学习框架，通过单token熵调节学习自适应思考，利用熵分析量化模型在关键决策token上决定是否启用思考的置信度，采用熵控奖励机制实现从高熵探索到低熵收敛的训练过程。

Result: 实验表明该方法使模型在复杂问题上保持准确性，同时在简单问题上提高效率，在多种文档任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: AdaThinking-E通过熵调节实现自适应思考，无需人工干预或外部难度标签，使模型能自主发现何时需要思考，平衡了准确性与效率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have demonstrated strong document reasoning capabilities by incorporating explicit thinking processes. While this capability significantly improves performance on challenging tasks, current models apply such deep reasoning uniformly to all questions, resulting in unnecessary computational overhead for simple task. This not only degrades user experience but also negatively impact accuracy on benchmark datasets. We identify the critical need for adaptive thinking mechanisms that can intelligently determine when to engage reasoning based on question complexity. To address this, we propose AdaThinking-E, a novel reinforcement learning framework that learns adaptive thinking through one-token entropy regulation. Our key insight is that model confidence in the decision to engage thinking (or not) can be quantified through entropy analysis of the predicted probability distribution at critical decision tokens. This observation motivates our entropy-governed reward mechanism: the training process naturally transitions from high-entropy exploration, where the model experiments with different thinking strategies, to low-entropy convergence with confident, generalizable decision-making policies. Crucially, this approach enables models to intrinsically discover when to think without requiring manual intervention or external difficulty labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach enables models to be both accurate on complex problems and efficient on simple ones across diverse document tasks.

</details>


### [62] [Position Is All You Need: A Free Lunch Token Compression Strategy for MLLM-based Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26142)
*Yuhan Liu,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在指代表达分割（RES）任务中计算开销过大的问题，提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的纯位置信息令牌压缩方法PAYN，该方法通过保留原始位置嵌入和局部空间结构，显著提升了压缩效率与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLM提升了RES性能，但其巨大的计算开销成为瓶颈，且现有令牌压缩方法在该任务上会导致严重的性能下降，亟需探究原因并设计专用压缩方案。

Method: 通过实验分析发现RES任务中视觉令牌的位置信息和局部邻域空间结构至关重要，据此提出PAYN方法：仅基于位置信息进行令牌压缩，严格保留原始位置索引，并确保令牌在局部邻域内的均匀分布，以维持空间关系一致性。

Result: 在多个RES基准测试中，PAYN优于现有令牌压缩方法，验证了在MLLM驱动的RES任务中，仅凭位置信息即可实现高效令牌压缩。

Conclusion: 位置信息是RES任务中令牌压缩的核心要素，PAYN证明了无需训练、仅依赖位置信息的压缩策略能有效平衡计算效率与模型性能。

Abstract: Referring Expression Segmentation (RES) aims to generate pixel-wise segmentation masks from complex and implicit textual queries. While recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have substantially boosted RES performance, their prohibitive computational overhead remains a critical bottleneck, which, however, is rarely explored. To fill this gap, we first evaluate typical token compression methods on this task and observe a surprising performance degradation. In this paper, we aim to understand this phenomenon for a solution. By extensive experiments, we find that token compression for RES requires preserving the original position embeddings and local neighboring spatial structures, indicating that visual token position information is far more critical than in other tasks. Building on this insight, we ask: Can we design the token compression method purely based on the position information? Therefore, we propose PAYN, a plug-and-play, training-free token compression method that relies solely on position information. PAYN retains tokens that are adequately distributed in every local neighboring region while strictly preserving original positional indices, thereby maintaining spatial relational consistency. Experiments on multiple RES benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing token compression methods, verifying that position is indeed all you need for token compression in the MLLM-based RES task. Codes are avaliable at https://github.com/YuhanLiu231/PAYN.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Accuracy: A Qualitative Analysis of Vision-Language Models for Hate Speech Detection in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26143)
*Muhammad Jawad Chowdhury,Adiba Hasan,Ishrak Hossain,Shahriar Ivan,Sabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文定性分析了四种先进的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在识别仇恨模因时的表现，发现它们虽在多模态任务上表现优异，但常忽略语境和反讽等关键线索，研究通过零样本和少样本提示评估了语境框架对模型输出及推理过程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模因因匿名性和病毒式传播成为散布仇恨的有力工具，但识别这种复杂且依赖语境的仇恨言论极具挑战；现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）往往忽略语境、反讽等细微线索，导致识别效果不佳。

Method: 对四种最先进VLMs（LLaVA-7B, Qwen-VL, GPT-4o mini, Claude 3 Haiku）进行定性分析，采用零样本（zero-shot）和少样本（few-shot）提示策略，评估语境框架如何影响模型输出，并深入分析模型生成的理由而非仅关注分类准确率。

Result: 揭示了VLMs在处理仇恨模因时的思维过程和局限性，表明语境框架显著影响模型的输出和推理能力，单纯的分类准确率不足以全面评估模型在复杂语境下的表现。

Conclusion: 通过定性评估模型生成的理由，能更深入理解VLMs在处理依赖语境的仇恨模因时的约束与机制，为改进模型识别复杂仇恨言论提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: Memes have turned out to be a powerful tool through which individuals share their ideas concerning contemporary social and political problems. Their anonymity, as well as their ability to go viral, make them a powerful medium for spreading hate. It remains very difficult to identify such complex and context-dependent hate speech. Although they display excellent performance on multimodal tasks, vision-language models (VLMs) tend to ignore context, irony, and other subtle cues that play a key role in identifying hateful memes. In this work, we present a qualitative analysis of four state-of-the-art VLMs: LLaVA-7B, Qwen-VL, GPT-4o mini, and Claude 3 Haiku. We evaluate these models under zero-shot and few-shot prompting to examine how contextual framing influences their outputs. Our analysis goes beyond simple classification accuracy and focuses on a qualitative evaluation of the models' generated justifications, providing a more in-depth understanding of their thought processes and constraints when dealing with hateful memes.

</details>


### [64] [Why Current XAI Is Not Enough for Arabic NLP: A Critical Survey of the Explainability Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26144)
*Salima Lamsiyah,Ruslan Mitkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对阿拉伯语可解释人工智能（XAI）的结构化综述，指出当前研究在方法、任务、语言三个维度存在不足，并提出语言本位的阿拉伯语XAI研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语NLP的可解释性研究滞后于主流NLP领域，存在方法单一（过度依赖LIME/SHAP等）、任务局限（集中于分类）、语言特性忽视（形态、方言、双言等）三大缺口，缺乏对阿拉伯语作为语言、文化、社会技术对象的忠实解释。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，系统梳理文本、语音及多模态场景下的阿拉伯语XAI文献，构建包含任务、方法、语言单位、变体、目标及评估实践的六维分类体系。

Result: 识别出阿拉伯语XAI在方法多样性、任务覆盖度及语言特性解释力方面的显著差距，现有研究未能充分处理阿拉伯语特有的形态学、方言变异、语码转换等复杂现象。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯语NLP不仅需要模型决策解释，更需要语言本位的解释框架；论文提出语言学 grounded 的阿拉伯语XAI研究议程，呼吁开发忠实于阿拉伯语语言文化特性的解释方法。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) is now a major theme in NLP; however, Arabic NLP remains under-explained in three connected senses. First, there is a method gap: Arabic XAI relies heavily on a small set of post-hoc techniques such as LIME, SHAP, attention visualization, and saliency, while broader NLP XAI offers richer diagnostic, counterfactual, probing, rationale-based, and human-centered methods. Second, there is a task gap: existing Arabic XAI work is concentrated in classification tasks, especially sentiment analysis, hate/offensive language detection, fake news, and spam, with weaker coverage of generation, retrieval, translation, summarization, structured prediction, and dialogue. Third, there is a linguistic gap: many explanations identify influential tokens, but rarely explain Arabic-specific phenomena such as morphology, clitics, dialectal variation, diglossia, orthographic ambiguity, diacritics, code-switching, named entities, cultural references, or Classical and religious registers. This critical structured survey synthesizes the reviewed literature on Arabic XAI across text, speech, and multimodal settings. We argue that Arabic NLP does not only need explanations of model decisions; it needs explanations that are faithful to Arabic as a linguistic, cultural, and sociotechnical object. We introduce a taxonomy of tasks, methods, linguistic units, varieties, goals, and evaluation practices, and propose a research agenda for linguistically grounded Arabic XAI.

</details>


### [65] [Vagdhenu: A Vrutta (Meter) Aware Shloka-to-Chant (TTS) System for Sanskrit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26146)
*Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Vagdhenu，一个针对梵文韵律（vrutta）感知的偈颂转吟诵系统。该系统并非提出新架构，而是基于现成的流匹配TTS骨干和神经声码器，通过添加特定的前端处理（解决施瓦删除、保留梵文音韵细节）和韵律感知机制，实现了高保真的梵文吟诵生成。研究发现文本侧韵律调节器在自填充流匹配架构中无效，需依赖参考音频和声音引导重训练。系统在多个模型家族的对比中表现出色，并已成功部署于视频语料库和音频应用中，同时开源了相关代码和数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用印度语TTS模型在处理梵文时存在严重缺陷，如受天城体影响导致的“施瓦删除”（schwa deletion）现象，以及无法准确处理梵文复杂的音韵规则（如visarga sandhi及其变体、送气对比、不同部位的嘶音区分）和韵律结构。因此，需要构建一个专门针对梵文吟诵（parayana recitation）的高保真系统，以忠实还原其特定的语音学和韵律特征。

Method: 采用现成的流匹配（flow-matching）TTS骨干网络和大尺度神经声码器作为基础。关键改进包括：1）前端路由：将梵文通过卡纳达语正字法处理，以规避印地语风格的施瓦删除；2）音韵前端：严格遵循梵文语音学规则，处理visarga sandhi的jihvamuliya和upadhmaniya同位异音，区分alpaprana/mahaprana送气对比及不同部位的嘶音；3）韵律感知机制：检测偈颂的韵律（vrutta），并在“半参考规则”下选择完全匹配的参考音频。此外，通过声音引导重训练（voice-steering retrain）而非文本侧调节器来控制韵律。

Result: 1）发现负面结果：在自填充流匹配骨干中，文本侧的韵律调节器在架构上是无效的（architecturally inert），因为模型能从上下文mel频谱恢复音高，导致嵌入无法获得梯度；参考音频片段和声音引导重训练是唯一有效的韵律控制手段。2）对比评估：在StyleTTS2、VITS2、Matcha-TTS和流匹配骨干四个模型家族的对比中，早期模型在连音或韵律上遇到瓶颈，而本系统（经过5小时克隆训练）在专家MOS评分中达到约4.6分。3）实际部署：已成功生成包含32章、5183个偈颂（约17.5小时）的视频语料库，以及覆盖12本书籍、约18000个偈颂的音频应用。

Conclusion: Vagdhenu系统证明了通过精心设计的特定语言前端和有效的韵律控制策略（参考音频与重训练），利用现有TTS架构即可实现高质量的梵文吟诵合成，而无需设计全新的模型架构。该研究强调了在处理低资源或高复杂度语言任务时，领域特定的预处理和正确的训练策略比模型架构创新更为关键。作者已开源前端、推理与训练代码、权重、单说话人吟诵数据集及交互式演示，促进了该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: We present Vagdhenu, a vrutta (meter) aware shloka-to-chant system for Sanskrit: a text-to-speech system that maps
  a metrical verse to its chanted parayana recitation at high fidelity. This is an experience report, not a new
  architecture. We take an off-the-shelf flow-matching TTS backbone and a large-scale neural vocoder, and add the
  components a faithful Sanskrit chant pipeline needs: a frontend that routes Sanskrit through Kannada orthography to
  avoid the Hindi-style schwa deletion that Devanagari triggers in Indic models; a frontend that obeys subtle
  Sanskrit phonology (visarga sandhi with its jihvamuliya and upadhmaniya allophones, the aspiration contrast of
  alpaprana and mahaprana, and the dental, retroflex, and palatal sibilants kept distinct); and a vrutta-aware
  mechanism that detects the meter and picks an exactly matched reference under a half-reference rule. We report a
  negative result that shaped the system: in a self-infilling flow-matching backbone, a text-side prosody conditioner
  is architecturally inert, because the model recovers pitch from the context mel and the embedding gets no
  gradient; the reference clip and a voice-steering retrain are the only working prosody levers. We also report a
  comparative lineage across four families (StyleTTS2, VITS2, Matcha-TTS, and the flow-matching backbone), where each
  earlier family hit a ceiling on conjuncts or prosody that a five-hour clone cleared at an expert MOS near 4.6. The
  system shipped two deployments: a 32-chapter, 5183-verse video corpus (about 17.5 hours) and an audio app covering
  about 18000 verses across 12 books. We release the frontend, inference and training code, weights, a
  single-speaker chant dataset, and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [66] [CARE: Causally-Aligned Reasoning Exploration for Medical Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26147)
*Yucheng Zhou,Peng Luo,Qianning Wang,Chengzhong Xu,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在医疗推理中因数据稀缺和标准强化学习缺陷导致的“答案对但推理错”问题，本文提出了CARE框架。该框架通过因果充分性和近端可学习性两个条件筛选高质量训练轨迹，并结合双流目标进行优化，显著提升了医疗推理的准确性和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决医疗领域专家标注数据稀缺且昂贵的问题，同时克服标准基于结果的强化学习方法在医疗场景中存在的自回归信用分配失败和梯度方差爆炸问题，避免模型陷入“答案正确但推理错误”的陷阱，即防止模型强化虚假相关性和数据集捷径而非有效的临床推导。

Method: 提出因果对齐推理探索（CARE）框架，包含两个核心条件：1. 因果充分性（Causal Sufficiency）：利用基于共识的自我验证机制模拟do-演算干预，有效消除梯度偏差；2. 近端可学习性（Proximal Learnability）：采用动态熵界选择模型“最近发展区”内的经验以实现方差有界的优化。筛选后的经验通过结合在线策略组相对探索与难度加权经验回放的双流目标函数进行优化。

Result: 在多样化的医疗多模态和纯文本基准测试中，CARE框架一致优于其他强力竞争对手，显著减少了“正确但不一致”的推理现象，并提高了训练稳定性。

Conclusion: CARE框架通过理论驱动的内部经验筛选和优化机制，有效解决了医疗LLM训练中的偏差和方差问题，为在数据稀缺环境下实现可靠、可解释的医疗推理提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for medical reasoning, yet the scarcity and cost of expert-annotated data constrain their progress. While reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative, standard outcome-based methods in medicine often suffer from autoregressive credit assignment failure and gradient variance explosion. This leads to the "Right Answer, Wrong Reason" trap, where models inadvertently reinforce spurious correlations and dataset shortcuts rather than valid clinical deduction. In this work, we propose Causally-Aligned Reasoning Exploration (CARE), a theoretically grounded framework for intrinsic experience curation. CARE is built upon two rigorous conditions for high-quality training trajectories: Causal Sufficiency, which utilizes an agreement-based self-verification mechanism to mimic $do$-calculus interventions and effectively debias gradients; and Proximal Learnability, which employs dynamic entropy bounds to select experiences within the model's zone of proximal development for variance-bounded optimization. These rigorously filtered experiences are optimized via a dual-stream objective that combines on-policy group-relative exploration with difficulty-weighted experience replay. Extensive experiments on diverse medical multimodal and text-only benchmarks demonstrate that CARE consistently outperforms other strong competitors, substantially reducing correct-but-inconsistent reasoning and improving training stability.

</details>


### [67] [Towards Interpretable Depression Detection: Linking Acoustic Features to DSM-5 Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26148)
*Jonas Länzlinger,Katharina O. E. Müller,Burkhard Stiller,Bruno Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种透明的链接框架，将语音声学特征与DSM-5抑郁行为指标联系起来，以实现可解释的抑郁症诊断，并在本地硬件上运行以保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症影响全球数百万人，但现有诊断依赖于可能遗漏真实行为的主观自我报告，需要更客观、可解释且保护隐私的诊断方法。

Method: 提出透明链接框架，明确将声学特征（音高变化、停顿、语速）映射到临床指标，系统可在普通硬件上本地运行。

Result: 在DAIC-WOZ数据集上的初步评估显示，声学特征与DSM-5中精神运动变化和注意力困难指标之间存在方向一致的关联。

Conclusion: 该框架支持可解释的抑郁症诊断设计原理，未来将在纵向数据集上验证并扩展多模态集成，同时保持边缘计算约束。

Abstract: Depression affects millions worldwide, yet diagnosis relies on subjective self-reports that may miss authentic behavior. This paper presents an approach linking speech acoustics to DSM-5 depressive-behavior indicators through a transparent Linkage Framework. Unlike black-box models, the framework explicitly maps acoustic features (pitch variability, pauses, speech tempo) to clinical indicators, enabling interpretable, indicator-level outputs. The system runs locally on commodity hardware (HW) to preserve privacy. Preliminary evaluation on DAIC-WOZ shows directionally consistent associations between acoustic features and DSM-5 indicators for psychomotor change and concentration difficulty, supporting the design rationale. Future work will validate on longitudinal datasets and extend multimodal integration while maintaining edge constraints.

</details>


### [68] [Artificial Intelligence Models Can Predict and Collaboratively Modulate Human Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26152)
*Eric Lacosse,Mariana Duarte,Graham Todd,Peter M. Todd,Daniel C. McNamee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究证明，大型语言模型（LLM）在语义流畅性任务中追踪和预测人类记忆轨迹的能力优于人类，表明LLM可作为增强人类发散性思维和创造性构思的认知工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在常规编码等任务中减少了人类输入的需求，但在需要开放式概念探索和创造性构思的任务中，其作为扩展人类能力的认知工具的潜力尚未被充分理解，特别是关于LLM如何增强人类生成性认知能力方面。

Method: 采用语义流畅性任务（SFT）这一经典认知范式，该任务要求生成性语义记忆检索，常用于表征人类的收敛性和发散性思维；研究在此框架下评估LLM跟随和增强人类思维轨迹的能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM在追踪和预测人类语义记忆轨迹方面的能力超过了其他人类参与者。

Conclusion: LLM不仅能在特定任务中替代人类努力，更具备作为认知工具增强人类生成性认知能力的潜力，特别是在人机交互中支持开放式探索和创意构思。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit unprecedented natural language generation and many text-based problem-solving capabilities. Indeed, in many language-based tasks, for example routine coding, these artificial intelligence models have reduced, or even eliminated, the need for human input. But rather than replacing human cognitive effort, LLMs may instead serve as cognitive tools to extend human abilities, particularly when they are engaged in a task requiring open-ended conceptual exploration and creative ideation. However, we are yet to understand how these models may enhance such generative human cognitive abilities in human--AI interactions. In this study, we explore and evaluate the ability of LLMs to follow and enhance human mental trajectories during semantic memory search. To test this, we use the semantic fluency task (SFT), a classic cognitive paradigm requiring generative semantic memory retrieval that has long served to characterize convergent and divergent thinking in humans. We demonstrate that an LLM's abilities to track and predict human memory trajectories in this task exceed those of other humans.

</details>


### [69] [Evaluating AI Generated Summaries for Cancer Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26154)
*Muhammad Aurangzeb Ahmad,Kim Shyu,Leon Oliver,Fergus Sleight,Paul Landau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了集成到癌症患者护理应用中的大语言模型（LLM）生成的医疗摘要，采用人类专家与“LLM即裁判”的双重评估框架，识别出摘要中存在偶尔遗漏和轻微不准确的问题，并据此优化了提示设计、依据 grounding 和安全护栏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能提升患者参与度和沟通效率，但其在临床环境中的准确性、忠实度和安全性仍引发担忧，亟需系统评估以确保医疗应用的安全可靠。

Method: 采用双重评估框架：一方面由肿瘤科临床医生和患者护理人员等人类领域专家对摘要的准确性、临床相关性和可读性进行真实值评估；另一方面利用大语言模型作为自动评估器（LLM-as-a-judge）进行并行分析。

Result: 发现生成的摘要存在偶尔的信息遗漏和轻微不准确问题，这些问题被系统分析并用于迭代改进提示工程、内容 grounding 机制及安全护栏策略。

Conclusion: 通过结合人类专家判断与LLM自动评估，可有效识别并修正AI生成医疗摘要中的缺陷，从而提升其在癌症患者护理应用中的可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into digital health platforms to generate summaries of complex medical data. Although these models can improve patient engagement and communication, these systems also raise concerns about accuracy, faithfulness, and safety in clinical contexts. In this study, we evaluate AI-generated summaries within a cancer patient care application using a dual assessment framework. Human domain experts, including oncology clinicians and patient-facing care staff, provided ground-truth evaluations of summary quality along dimensions of accuracy, clinical relevance, and readability. In parallel, we employed LLMs serving as evaluators (LLM-as-a-judge). Some limitations were identified in the generated summaries e.g., occasional omissions and minor inaccuracies. These were systematically analyzed and used to iteratively improve prompt design, grounding, and safety guardrails.

</details>


### [70] [VFA: Empowering Multilingual MLLMs via Vision-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26155)
*Yixia Li,Yaqing Shi,Zhiwen Ruan,Dongdong Zhang,Lingjie Jiang,Shaohan Huang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-Free Adaptation (VFA) 框架，通过任务向量融合技术，在无需多模态监督数据的情况下，利用多语言文本数据提升多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的多语言能力，有效解决了数据稀缺和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型多以英语为中心，扩展多语言能力受限于高质量非英语图文监督数据的稀缺与高昂成本；而直接利用丰富的多语言文本数据进行微调，往往会破坏视觉-语言对齐并导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出Vision-Free Adaptation (VFA) 框架，将多语言增强与视觉对齐解耦。具体方法是在共享LLM骨干网络上，通过在多语言文本数据上微调得到多语言任务向量，并将其与MLLM的视觉对齐任务向量进行融合（composition）。

Result: 在5个MLLM模型和6个多语言多模态基准测试中，VFA均展现出一致的性能提升，同时保留了通用的多模态和纯文本能力。此外，仅使用不到2%的文本数据，VFA即可显著缩小与全量多模态训练模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: VFA是一种高效的数据利用策略，成功实现了多语言能力提升与视觉对齐保持的平衡，为低成本构建多语言多模态模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have advanced rapidly, yet most remain English-centric, as scaling multilingual multimodal instruction tuning is limited by the scarcity and high cost of high-quality non-English image-text supervision. Although multilingual text data is abundant, naive textual fine-tuning can disrupt vision-language alignment and induce catastrophic forgetting. We propose Vision-Free Adaptation (VFA), a framework that decouples multilingual language enhancement from visual alignment by composing complementary task vectors over a shared LLM backbone. Specifically, we fine-tune a base LLM on multilingual text data to derive a multilingual task vector, which is then merged with the vision-aligned task vector of an MLLM. Experiments on five MLLMs across six multilingual multimodal benchmarks show consistent improvements while preserving both general multimodal and text-only capabilities. Moreover, using less than 2% of the text data, VFA narrows the gap to the fully multimodal-trained model, demonstrating its data efficiency.

</details>


### [71] [Self-Generated Text Recognition: Quality Heuristics, Cross-Task Transfer, and Downstream Bias in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26159)
*Jesse St. Amand,Callum Canavan,Sohaib Imran,Joseph Hewson,Aaron Lutz,Shi Feng,Puria Radmard,Lennie Wells*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）识别自身生成文本的能力（SGTR），发现评估格式、对话结构和任务领域等实验设计选择显著影响结果，且质量启发式是主要混淆因素；训练可提升SGTR能力并导致模型在评判时偏好自身输出，建议在设计安全关键AI应用时监控SGTR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGTR能力可能对依赖LLM作为评估者或监控器的AI安全机制构成风险，例如模型可能识别其他同模型副本的输出并产生偏见判断或共谋；先前研究对此能力是否存在结论冲突，需 reconcile 这些发现。

Method: 在六种操作化（operationalizations）设置下评估13-21个模型，考察评估格式（成对vs单独评估）、对话结构（用户标签vs助手标签呈现文本）和任务领域（如编码vs摘要）对SGTR准确率的影响；通过监督微调（SFT）提升SGTR性能并测试泛化性；在AlpacaEval框架中测试训练后模型的评判偏好。

Result: SGTR准确率随评估格式、对话结构和任务领域显著变化；质量启发式（模型将高质量文本归因于自身）是主要混淆因素；SFT提升SGTR性能可泛化到其他评估配置；训练SGTR导致模型在AlpacaEval中更偏好自身输出。

Conclusion: 尽管存在混淆因素，部分模型具备实用的SGTR能力；在一种设置中训练SGTR会影响更广泛的自识别和自偏好行为；应在安全关键AI应用设计中监控并考虑SGTR。

Abstract: Self-Generated Text Recognition (SGTR)--the ability of an LLM to identify its own outputs--poses risks to AI safeguards that rely on LLMs as evaluators or monitors. Specifically, an LLM may recognize outputs from other copies of the same model and make biased judgments or collude outright. Prior work has drawn conflicting conclusions about whether current models possess significant SGTR capabilities. We reconcile these findings by identifying key experimental design choices--which we term operationalizations--that drive divergent results. Evaluating 13-21 models across six operationalizations, we find that accuracy varies substantially with evaluation format (pairwise vs. individual assessments of text), conversation structure (presenting candidate text in user tags vs. assistant tags), and the domain of the task used to generate candidate text (e.g., coding vs. summarization). We corroborate previous observations that a quality heuristic--models attributing authorship to text they perceive as higher quality--is a dominant confound.
