The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [108] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将“可量化性”（即能否获得有效的效价估计）作为独立于“生物活性”的筛选分类目标，发现基于筛选响应特征的模型能准确预测可量化性，从而优化昂贵的剂量反应分析资源的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的高通量药物筛选策略隐含假设确认的生物活性必然能转化为可量化的效价估计，但实际上许多活性化合物无法产生可报告的剂量反应曲线，导致昂贵的后续验证资源浪费。

Method: 建立了一个建模“可量化性”的框架，将其作为独立的分类目标；利用低成本初筛中的观测响应特征（而非分子结构）训练预测器，并在未见过的化学支架和保留的 assay 机制家族中验证其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 可量化性具有高度可预测性，主要信息来源于筛选响应特征；预测器在新化学支架和不同 assay 机制间表现稳健；成功量化的概率与响应振幅和 assay 背景强相关。

Conclusion: 实验可测量性是独立于生物活性的可预测属性，引入“可量化性感知”的筛选分类策略能显著提升昂贵剂量反应分析资源的分配效率。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [109] [Chart2SVG: Editable SVG Generation from Raster Chart Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26544)
*Jinning Cui,Lu Chen,Haoyan Shi,Yue He,Chenglong Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Weidong Huang,Yunhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chart2SVG，一个将静态光栅图表转换为可程序编辑的语义增强SVG的多模态大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将静态图表转换为既视觉准确又结构一致的可编辑SVG，限制了可视化的高级操作和重用。

Method: 通过在视觉语言模型中引入图表特定语义标记，结合Beagle+数据集（33K样本）、专门训练目标及渲染感知后训练阶段，并构建图表结构图（CSG）以暴露视觉依赖。

Result: 实验表明Chart2SVG在重建保真度和下游编辑实用性方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法推动了智能和交互式可视化工具的发展，实现了图表的交互式探索、重新利用和布局重用。

Abstract: We present Chart2SVG, a multimodal large language model that converts static raster charts into structurally organized, semantically enriched SVGs that support programmatic editing. By incorporating chart-specific semantic tokens into a vision-language model, Chart2SVG captures both geometric primitives and their functional roles. To support robust structural recovery, we introduce Beagle+, a dataset of 33K canonicalized and structurally distilled chart samples. Our approach combines specialized training objectives with a rendering-aware post-training phase, producing SVGs that are both visually accurate and structurally consistent. To facilitate higher-level manipulations, we construct a Chart Structure Graph (CSG) that exposes visual dependencies, enabling tasks such as interactive exploration, chart repurposing, and layout reuse. Experiments show that Chart2SVG substantially outperforms baselines in reconstruction fidelity and downstream editing utility, advancing the development of intelligent and interactive visualization tools.

</details>


### [110] [Arrive and Survive: Scaling Safe Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning from One-Bit Failure Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26571)
*Guopeng Li,Yiyang Duan,Yiru Jiao,Chengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对失败终止的马尔可夫决策过程中，现有对比强化学习（CRL）因忽略失败概率质量而导致价值高估和不安全策略的问题，提出了安全对比强化学习（Safe-CRL）。该方法通过质量加权InfoNCE和对数生存质量评分两项修正，仅需失败终止信号即可实现安全的目标条件策略学习，在多个机器人任务中显著提升了生存率和目标达成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CRL在构建正样本时仅考虑失败前的未来目标，忽略了因失败终止而移除的概率质量。这种遗漏导致目标到达价值的系统性高估偏差，使得接近失败的轨迹获得了与其实际未来占用率不成比例的强监督信号，进而通过灾难性失败自举强化了不安全动作，导致策略学习失败和行为不可持续。

Method: 提出Safe-CRL框架，包含两项核心修正：1) 质量加权InfoNCE（mass-weighted InfoNCE），用于校正评论家学习中短生存未来的过度加权；2) 对数生存质量评分（log-survival-mass score），用于在策略优化中恢复缺失的生存质量。该方法仅需失败终止提供的单比特信号。

Result: 在12个易失败的机器人导航和运动任务中，Safe-CRL一致提高了生存率，并在目标达成性能上大幅优于Scaling-CRL基线。此外，深度Safe-CRL策略展现了复杂的避障行为。

Conclusion: 该研究完善了失败终止条件下的CRL理论，并提供了一个可扩展的安全强化学习框架，有效解决了因忽略失败概率质量导致的策略不安全问题。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) scales effectively in goal-conditioned tasks by casting policy learning into a self-supervised contrastive objective. However, in a failure-terminated Markov decision process, established CRL considers pre-failure future goals only when constructing positive samples, without accounting for the probability mass removed by failure termination. Our theoretical analysis shows that this omission induces a systematic overestimation bias in goal-reaching values. Consequently, near-failure trajectories provide disproportionately strong supervision of success despite retaining little future occupancy. Unsafe actions can thereby be reinforced through catastrophic failure bootstrapping, leading to failed policy learning and unsustainable goal-reaching behaviours. To address this problem, we introduce two minimal yet strong corrections: mass-weighted InfoNCE corrects the overweighting of short surviving futures in critic learning, and a log-survival-mass score restores the missing survival mass in policy optimization. The resulting method, Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL), requires only the one-bit signal provided by failure termination to scale safe goal-conditioned policy learning. Across twelve failure-prone robot navigation and locomotion tasks, Safe-CRL consistently improves survival and substantially outperforms the Scaling-CRL baseline in goal-reaching performance. Additionally, deep Safe-CRL policies exhibit complex failure-avoidance behaviours. This study completes the CRL theory under failure termination and provides a scalable safe RL framework. The code is available via https://github.com/RomainLITUD/safe-crl.

</details>


### [111] [Activation Outliers Matter: Robust Recovery for Quantized Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26581)
*Tanzila Rahman,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Zhuang Ma,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Yao Wang,Xing Huang,Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Yonghan Dong,Wei Guo,Yaoyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的低位量化方案，发现激活量化是性能下降的主要原因，并提出残差回退量化（RFQ）框架来恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低位量化（如MXFP4、HiF4）可降低MLLMs的计算和内存需求，但激进量化导致性能显著下降，需找出性能损失来源并提出解决方案。

Method: 系统性研究不同量化方案在视频生成和推理任务中的表现，通过消融实验识别激活量化为主要瓶颈，提出Residual Fallback Quantization（RFQ）框架，通过辅助量化残差路径补偿量化误差。

Result: MXFP8接近无损，4-bit量化导致显著性能下降；RFQ在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上恢复了MXFP4和HiF4量化损失的大部分性能，缩小了与BF16基线的差距。

Conclusion: 激活量化是超低位MLLMs的主要瓶颈，基于残差的激活重建是实现鲁棒4-bit部署的有效策略。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization offers a promising avenue for reducing the computational and memory demands of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that MXFP8 achieves near-lossless performance, whereas aggressive 4-bit quantization leads to significant degradation. Through extensive ablations, we identify activation quantization as the primary source of this performance loss, contributing substantially more than weight quantization. Motivated by this observation, we propose Residual Fallback Quantization (RFQ), a lightweight activation reconstruction framework that supplements the primary ulta-low-bit activation representation with an auxiliary quantized residual pathway. By explicitly modeling and compensating for quantization errors, RFQ improves activation fidelity while preserving the efficiency advantages of ultra-low-bit computation. RFQ requires no architectural modifications and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Wan2.2 and Qwen3-VL demonstrate that RFQ consistently recovers a substantial portion of the performance lost under the quantization of MXFP4 and HiF4, significantly narrowing the gap to BF16 baselines across both generation and 4 reasoning benchmarks. Our findings establish activation quantization as the dominant bottleneck in ultra-low-bit MLLMs and highlight residual-based activation reconstruction as an effective and practical strategy for robust 4-bit deployment.

</details>


### [112] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了J-Zero框架，通过挑战者、求解者和评判者的协同进化，解决了不可验证领域中自进化语言模型的研究空白，在两类领域均显著优于基线且能持续迭代提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化语言模型虽能降低人工监督成本并在可验证领域取得进展，但在不可验证领域的自进化研究仍严重不足，缺乏统一的框架支持跨领域的自我提升。

Method: 提出J-Zero（从零数据开始的评判者协同适应）框架，包含三个协同进化组件：挑战者生成日益困难的任务，求解者学习生成高质量响应，两者通过对抗交互共进；评判者则利用预先已知排序的偏好对（如求解者答案优于挑战者答案、分解重组答案优于单次生成答案）进行协同适应，而非依赖自身评分。

Result: 在可验证领域平均超越基线4.2分，在不可验证领域平均超越基线8.0分；J-Zero在至少十次迭代中持续改进，而基线方法在两次迭代后性能即开始下降。

Conclusion: J-Zero框架成功实现了可验证与不可验证领域的统一自进化，通过挑战者 - 求解者的对抗机制和评判者的先验偏好协同适应，有效克服了现有方法在迭代稳定性和不可验证任务上的局限，为迈向超级智能提供了更具扩展性的路径。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them. In parallel, the Judge co-adapts using preference pairs whose ordering is known in advance from how each response was produced, i.e., the Solver's answer over the Challenger's, and its decomposed-and-recombined answer over its one-shot answer, rather than from the Judge's own scores. J-Zero outperforms the baselines by an average of 4.2 points on verifiable and 8.0 points on unverifiable domains, and continues to improve through at least ten iterations, whereas the baselines degrade after two.

</details>


### [113] [SimCast-S2S: An Efficient Generative Model for Subseasonal Precipitation Forecasting via Transfer Learning from Climate Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26594)
*Hiep V. Dang,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SimCast-S2S，一种基于潜在扩散模型的生成式框架，用于解决次季节至季节（S2S）降水预报中不确定性量化难、计算成本高及训练数据不足三大瓶颈。该方法在潜在空间运行以降低计算量，并利用低秩自适应（LoRA）迁移学习策略，在仅使用部分大气变量且无后处理的情况下，性能优于现有深度学习基线，并与ECMWF等顶尖业务系统相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S降水预报具有重要的经济和社会影响，但受限于预测信号弱、不确定性高以及业务系统计算成本高昂导致模拟保真度受限。现有的数据驱动方法难以有效进行概率预报（不确定性量化），且在物理空间生成大规模集合计算代价过大，同时扩散模型通常依赖海量训练数据，而高质量的重分析数据有限。

Method: 提出SimCast-S2S框架，结合三大核心技术：1. 采用基于扩散的生成式流水线，从条件分布中有效采样以实现概率预报；2. 利用变分自编码器（VAE）学习紧凑的潜在空间，在潜在空间而非物理空间进行扩散过程，以实现高效的大规模集合生成；3. 引入带有低秩自适应（LoRA）的迁移学习策略，先在大规模气候模拟集合上预训练，再在有限的重分析数据上微调。

Result: 在重分析数据上，SimCast-S2S的表现优于包括卷积神经网络（CNN）和U-Net在内的深度学习基线。尽管仅使用部分大气输入变量且未进行任何后处理、偏差校正或校准，该系统仍能与ECMWF-S2S等最先进的业务系统竞争，并在许多情况下表现更佳。

Conclusion: 潜在生成建模与“模拟到重分析”迁移学习的结合，为数据驱动的概率性S2S降水预报提供了一条高效且可扩展的路径，能够有效突破当前预报系统在不确定性量化和计算效率方面的瓶颈。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) precipitation forecasting has substantial financial and societal impact, yet remains challenging because of weak predictive signals, high associated uncertainty, and the computational cost of operational systems, which constrains simulation fidelity. We introduce SimCast-S2S, a generative latent-diffusion framework for probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting that addresses three major bottlenecks in data-driven prediction. First, because S2S prediction requires uncertainty quantification rather than only deterministic point forecasts, SimCast-S2S is the first data-driven system that uses a diffusion-based generative pipeline for S2S prediction, enabling effective sampling from the underlying conditional distribution. Second, since generating large probabilistic ensembles is computationally costly in physical space, SimCast-S2S instead operates in a compact latent space learned by variational autoencoders, enabling efficient large-ensemble generation. Third, diffusion models typically require large training datasets; SimCast-S2S overcomes this via transfer learning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), pretraining on large ensembles of climate simulations before fine-tuning on limited reanalysis data. On reanalysis data, SimCast-S2S outperforms deep learning baselines, including convolutional neural networks and U-Net architectures. Notably, despite using only a subset of atmospheric input variables and no post-processing, bias correction, or calibration, SimCast-S2S remains competitive with, and in many cases outperforms, state-of-the-art operational systems such as the ECMWF-S2S baseline. These results indicate that latent generative modeling combined with simulation-to-reanalysis transfer learning offers an efficient and scalable path toward data-driven probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [114] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对多种机器学习模型在可再生能源场景下的预测性能进行了全面基准测试，发现不同模型在不同数据结构下表现各异，其中混合集成模型表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对多种AI架构在互补可再生能源场景下的系统性基准测试，需要明确不同模型在结构化WEC数据和时空交互场景下的适用性。

Method: 使用三个数据集（大规模WEC数据集、16 WEC数据集、Penmanshiel风电场SCADA测量数据），对比传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、图模型和混合集成深度学习方法的性能。

Result: Extra Trees在结构化WEC数据上表现最佳，MAE降低63.7%；STGCN在时空交互场景下MAE为167.0 kW，R=0.93；RF BiLSTM混合模型整体预测精度最高，MAE=150.5 kW，比独立LSTM降低75%，比STGCN提升10%。

Conclusion: 没有单一AI架构是普遍最优的：随机和boosted集成适合结构化WEC代理建模，图网络在空间交互主导时更优，集成循环混合模型在非线性表格关系和时序动态共存时表现最强。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


### [115] [Robust Neural Stimulation Response Modeling Through Meta-Learning and Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26649)
*Matthew J Bryan,Daniel C Muir,Felix Schwock,Azadeh Yazdan-Shahmorad,Rajesh P N Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将元学习（MAML）和跨会话预训练应用于神经刺激响应建模，显著降低了预测灾难性失败率，并将校准数据需求减少了50-90%，解决了基于模型的闭环神经刺激在临床部署中的关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的闭环神经刺激在治疗帕金森病和感觉恢复等方面具有潜力，但其部署受到两个主要障碍的限制：一是预测模型在部分会话中会出现灾难性的预测失败；二是每次会话所需的校准过程往往不符合临床时间约束。

Method: 研究扩展了时序基函数模型（TBFMs），提出了一种基于模型无关元学习（MAML）的新型架构和算法，利用跨会话预训练来预测状态依赖的神经刺激响应。该方法在两只非人灵长类动物的初级感觉运动皮层进行的40次光遗传刺激会话中进行了评估。

Result: 元学习显著减少了灾难性预测失败：在1k校准集大小下，测试R平方小于0.05的会话数从单会话训练的16次降至1次；预测区间显著变窄（p < 0.05）。在保持同等精度的前提下，校准需求降低了50-90%，使得原本受限于临床时间的实验变得可行。

Conclusion: 研究证明刺激响应中存在一致的跨会话结构，足以支持预训练，首次提供了元学习方法适用于神经刺激的经验证据。其鲁棒性和样本效率的提升直接解决了部署障碍，并呼吁建立标准化的多站点刺激数据集以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Objective: Model-based closed-loop neural stimulation holds promise for therapeutic applications ranging from Parkinson's disease to sensory restoration, but deployment has been limited by two obstacles: 1) forecasting models for predicting the consequences of stimulation fail catastrophically on a meaningful fraction of sessions, and 2) per-session calibration requirements are often incompatible with clinical constraints. We address both by demonstrating, for the first time, that meta-learning and pretraining can be applied to neural stimulation response modeling. Methods: Temporal basis function models (TBFMs) forecast state-dependent neural responses to stimulation. We extend TBFMs with cross-session pretraining using a novel architecture and algorithm based on model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML), evaluating them on 40 sessions of optogenetic stimulation in primary sensorimotor cortex of two non-human primates. Results: Meta-learning substantially reduces catastrophic forecast failure: for a 1k calibration set size, sessions with test R-squared < 0.05 drop from 16 of 40 (single-session training) to 1 (MAML-pretrained), and prediction intervals become significantly narrower (p < 0.05). Calibration requirements are reduced by 50-90% at matched accuracy, enabling experiments otherwise infeasible within clinical session-time constraints. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate that cross-session structure in stimulation responses is consistent enough to support pretraining, providing the first empirical evidence that meta-learning approaches are viable for neural stimulation. Significance: The robustness and sample efficiency gains directly address known obstacles to deploying model-based stimulation controllers. Our results motivate community efforts to assemble standardized multi-site stimulation datasets and to further explore meta-learning for robust closed-loop stimulation.

</details>


### [116] [When Privacy Hurts Mergeability: Geometry-Aware Model Merging under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26655)
*Jin Liu,Junkang Liu,Ning Xi,Yinbin Miao,Dawei Wei,Ke Cheng,Jianfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了差分隐私模型合并的几何特性，发现局部锐度和参考漂移两个障碍，并提出DP-Merging框架来改善私有任务模型的可合并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可以在不访问原始任务数据的情况下构建多任务模型，但发布的任务模型可能泄露隐私数据。差分隐私可以限制这种泄露，但其对模型合并的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 提出DP-Merging框架，使用DP兼容的锐度感知目标引导私有任务模型向更平坦的损失区域移动，并使用基于参考的对齐正则化器使任务模型保持接近共享的预训练初始化。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务上的实验表明，DP-Merging在不同隐私预算下持续提高了私有合并模型的性能，同时保留了底层DP微调过程的隐私保证。

Conclusion: 通过减少局部曲率和参考漂移，可以收紧合并引起的损失增加的上界，DP-Merging框架有效改善了差分隐私任务模型的可合并性。

Abstract: Model merging promises to construct a single multi-task model from independently fine-tuned task models without accessing the original task data. This makes it attractive when task data cannot be centralized, but released task models may still leak private fine-tuning data. Differential privacy (DP) provides a principled mechanism for limiting such leakage, yet its effect on model merging remains poorly understood. In this paper, we study the geometry of differentially private model merging and identify two geometric obstacles that make private task models difficult to merge: \emph{local sharpness}, which makes task losses sensitive to the parameter displacement induced by merging, and \emph{reference drift}, which measures the displacement of private task models from the shared pretrained initialization and amplifies cross-task interference. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{DP-Merging}, a geometry-aware framework that improves the mergeability of differentially private task models. DP-Merging uses a DP-compatible sharpness-aware objective to guide each private task model toward flatter loss regions, and a reference-based alignment regularizer to keep task models close to the shared pretrained initialization. We derive a merge-gap upper bound showing that reducing local curvature and reference drift tightens the bound on the loss increase induced by merging. Experiments on vision and language tasks across multiple privacy budgets show that DP-Merging consistently improves private merged-model performance while preserving the privacy guarantees of the underlying DP fine-tuning procedures.

</details>


### [117] [Simple Actors and Deep Critics for Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26659)
*Guhyeon Kang,Jaehwi Lee,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAC（轻演员、深评论家）架构，通过将计算容量从部署时的演员转移到仅训练时使用的评论家，解决了离线强化学习中生成式演员推理延迟高的问题，在保持性能的同时显著降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习生成式演员（如扩散模型和流匹配策略）虽然能捕捉多模态行为，但每次决策都需要多次去噪或积分步骤，导致部署时推理开销巨大。由于评论家仅在训练阶段使用，而演员在每次决策时运行，因此将模型容量分配给评论家而非演员更能优化推理效率，但加深评论家网络会引发稳定性问题。

Method: 作者识别了加深评论家网络时的三种失效模式（优化困难、自举噪声放大、值范围漂移），并针对性地提出了三种解决方案：残差MLP骨干网络、n步自举目标以及分类交叉熵损失。结合这些改进与轻量级确定性演员，构建了LAC方法。

Result: 在OGBench基准测试中，LAC的性能匹配了最强的扩散和流匹配基线，同时实现了高达4倍的推理延迟降低，其效果可与无需蒸馏的一步蒸馏策略相媲美。此外，该评论家设计可迁移至不同的演员参数化形式。

Conclusion: 通过重新分配离线演员 - 评论家方法中的容量，利用深度评论家配合轻量级演员，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升离线强化学习的部署效率，为高效推理提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Recent progress in offline reinforcement learning (RL) has been driven by expressive generative actors such as diffusion and flow-matching policies, which capture multimodal behavior in offline datasets. However, these actors require multiple denoising or integration steps per action and thus incur substantial overhead at every decision in deployment. In this work, we revisit where capacity should be invested in an offline actor--critic method. Since the critic is used only during training and is discarded at deployment while the actor runs at every decision step, allocating capacity to the critic rather than the actor is more favorable for inference-time efficiency. However, scaling MLP critics in offline RL is known to introduce several distinct instabilities that have, in practice, kept critics shallow. We identify three distinct failure modes that arise when critics are deepened in offline RL---optimization, bootstrap-noise amplification, and value-range drift---and address each with a corresponding ingredient: a residual MLP backbone, n-step bootstrap targets, and a categorical cross-entropy loss. Combining these ingredients with a lightweight deterministic actor, we propose LAC (Light Actor, deep Critic). On OGBench, LAC matches the strongest diffusion- and flow-matching baselines while achieving up to 4x lower inference latency, comparable to one-step distilled policies without distillation. Its critic recipe also transfers across actor parametrizations.

</details>


### [118] [Neural Regression with Embeddings for Numerical Attribute Prediction in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26729)
*Rupesh Sapkota,Louis Mozart Kamdem Teyou,Moshood Yekini,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LitEm的神经回归模型，使传递性知识图谱嵌入模型能够预测数值属性，并进一步提出了一种联合训练框架，在提升链接预测性能（尤其是双线性模型）的同时，赋予模型预测数值属性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管知识图谱通常包含丰富的数值属性，但现有的大多数传递性知识图谱嵌入模型忽略了这些信息，限制了其对现实世界多样化知识图谱的表示能力。

Method: 1. 提出LitEm神经回归模型，用于在知识图谱内预测数值属性；2. 设计联合训练框架，将先进的传递性嵌入模型与LitEm共同训练，以同时优化链接预测和属性预测任务。

Result: 在FB15K-237、YAGO15K、DB15K和Mutagenesis数据集上，LitEm在大多数属性上取得了最佳或次佳结果；联合训练显著提升了双线性模型的链接预测性能，并通过“字面感知”评估证实模型有效编码并利用了属性信息，而非仅依赖参数量增加。

Conclusion: 引入数值属性预测机制及联合训练框架，能有效增强知识图谱嵌入模型对现实世界复杂信息的表达能力和任务性能，且代码已开源。

Abstract: In recent years, transductive knowledge graph embedding models have been applied to tasks such as link prediction and query answering. Although knowledge graphs often contain rich numerical attributes, most embedding models neglect them, limiting their ability to represent real-world knowledge graphs with diverse information. In this work, we propose a neural regression model (LitEm) that enables transductive knowledge graph embedding models to predict numerical attributes within knowledge graphs. Experimental results demonstrate that LitEm achieves the best or second-best results on most attributes across FB15K-237, YAGO15K, DB15K, and Mutagenesis. Furthermore, we propose a co-training framework that jointly trains state-of-the-art transductive knowledge graph embedding models with LitEm, which improves link prediction performance mainly for bilinear models and simultaneously enables them to predict numerical attributes. In addition, the literal-awareness evaluation demonstrates that co-training helps models to encode and exploit attribute information in a "literal-aware'' manner, suggesting that the observed gains are not merely due to additional parameters. We publicly release our implementation at https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings.

</details>


### [119] [Rethinking Message Passing as Retrieval for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26732)
*Jintang Li,Yuhong Chen,Ruofan Wu,Binli Luo,Jiayi Ji,Hui Li,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RTA框架，将图神经网络（GNN）重构为检索增强视图，用基于标签的检索与传播替代传统的结构消息传递，在保持或超越GNN及图大模型性能的同时，显著提升了效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统消息传递范式计算成本高、对图结构缺陷敏感的问题，并解释为何邻域聚合通常优于节点级多层感知机（MLP）。

Method: 提出RTA框架，采用检索增强视角：每层利用MLP结合节点表示与检索到的图上下文的排列不变摘要进行预测；用标签感知的检索和传播机制替代结构化的消息传递。

Result: 在多个文本属性图基准测试中，RTA的性能匹配甚至超越了强力的GNN和图大模型基线，同时在多样场景下展现了更高的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 检索增强的聚合机制在理论上可关联到softmax注意力消息传递，且对检索错误的异常值具有鲁棒性；RTA证明了基于检索的简单MLP框架能有效替代复杂的消息传递网络。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically conceptualized as message-passing neural networks, yet it remains unclear why neighborhood aggregation reliably outperforms node-wise multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Despite its empirical success, this paradigm can be computationally expensive and sensitive to imperfect graph structures. In this work, we present a retrieval-augmented view of GNNs: each layer makes predictions by applying an MLP to a node representation together with a permutation-invariant summary of retrieved graph context. Motivated by this perspective, we propose RTA, a simple MLP-based framework that replaces structural message passing with label-aware retrieval and propagation. We provide theoretical insights that (i) connect retrieval-based aggregation to softmax-attention message passing, and (ii) establish the robustness of retrieved-context supervision to mis-retrieved outliers. Experiments on multiple text-attributed graph benchmarks show that RTA matches or even outperforms strong GNN and graph LLM baselines while improving efficiency and robustness across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Augmented Diffusion Guidance for Physics-Informed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26748)
*Akira Osaka,Naoya Takeishi,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散引导和自生成数据增强的物理信息扩散模型，通过将物理偏差设为零来生成符合物理定律的时空信号，无需在去噪过程中求解控制方程，显著降低了生成结果与真实动力学的偏差并提升了生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型在生成物理现象（如流体动力学）的时空信号时，缺乏对底层物理定律约束的整合，导致生成样本虽然视觉上合理，但在动力学上可能严重偏离真实情况。

Method: 提出一种物理信息方法，利用扩散引导结合自生成数据增强，学习以物理偏差程度为条件的数据分布；在采样时显式将偏差条件设为零，从而解耦控制方程评估与扩散模型的训练及采样过程，避免在每次去噪迭代中求解控制方程。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比标准扩散模型显著减少了生成样本的物理偏差，且在与现有物理约束扩散方法结合时，能进一步降低偏差，同时适用于计算昂贵的数值模拟问题并实现更快的样本生成。

Conclusion: 该方法通过解耦物理方程求解与扩散过程，在保持生成效率的同时有效整合物理约束，为生成符合物理定律的复杂时空信号提供了一种简单而高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models can be used to generate spatiotemporal signals of physical phenomena, such as time-series images of fluid dynamics. However, a major limitation of standard diffusion models is that they do not incorporate constraints derived from the underlying physical laws. Consequently, generated samples may appear visually plausible while deviating substantially from the true dynamics. In this study, we propose a simple yet effective physics-informed approach based on diffusion guidance with self-generated data augmentation. The proposed method learns the data distribution conditioned on the degree of deviation from the physically correct dynamics and generates samples by explicitly setting the deviation condition to be zero. The method decouples the evaluation of the governing equations from the diffusion model training and sampling processes, avoiding the need to solve the governing equations at every iteration of the denoising process. This design makes the method applicable to problems requiring computationally expensive numerical simulations and enables faster sample generation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model not only significantly reduces the deviations compared with standard diffusion models but also achieves further reductions when combined with existing physics-constrained diffusion methods.

</details>


### [121] [Safety by Design: Realized-Cost Constraints for Contextual Bandits with Continuous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26755)
*Spyros Dragazis,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高概率约束的上下文多臂老虎机算法（High-Probability Constrained UCB），用于在异方差环境下确保决策安全性，通过乐观 - 悲观策略在探索奖励的同时保守估计安全动作集，实现了紧致的后悔界并显著减少了成本违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全约束老虎机模型通常要求每个动作的期望成本低于阈值，但在异方差设置中，动作不仅影响期望奖励和成本，还影响观测结果的变异性，仅约束期望成本可能不足以保证安全，特别是在剂量选择或自动驾驶等单次不安全决策可能导致灾难性后果的应用中。

Method: 提出 High-Probability Constrained UCB 算法，采用乐观 - 悲观策略：对奖励进行乐观估计以探索，对成本进行悲观估计以保守确定安全动作集；针对线性和一般函数类模型分别使用线性回归和 eluder 维度进行分析。

Result: 证明了线性奖励和成本模型下紧致的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$后悔界，并将分析扩展到一般函数类；实验表明，与期望成本约束基线相比，强制实现成本安全显著减少了违规次数。

Conclusion: 在异方差环境下，采用阶段性的实现成本高概率约束比传统的期望成本约束更能有效保障安全性，所提算法在理论保证和实验效果上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Contextual bandits are a standard framework for sequential decision-making under uncertainty, with applications in clinical trials, dosage selection, recommendation systems, and autonomous systems. Safety is central in many of these applications, since a single unsafe decision in settings such as dosage selection or autonomous driving can have catastrophic consequences. A common way to model safety in bandit problems is to associate each action with both a reward signal and a cost signal, and to optimize reward subject to constraints on cost. Most existing safety-constrained bandit models enforce safety by requiring the expected cost of each action to remain below a prescribed threshold. However, this may be insufficient in heteroscedastic settings, where the chosen action affects not only the expected reward and cost, but also the variability of the observed outcomes. We study contextual bandits with one-dimensional continuous actions and stage-wise high-probability constraints on the realized cost. We propose High-Probability Constrained UCB, an optimistic-pessimistic algorithm that explores for reward while conservatively estimating the safe action set. For linear reward and cost models, we prove a tight $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{T})$ regret bound, and we extend the analysis to general function classes using the eluder dimension. Experiments show that enforcing realized-cost safety substantially reduces violations compared with expected-cost constrained baselines.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Client Averaging: A Client-Independent Second-Order Stationary-Bias Component in Stochastic SCAFFOLD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26765)
*Yi-Ping Tang,Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在全参与、一维同质客户端的随机 Scaf 算法中，客户端平均虽能消除主导的 $O(\gamma/N)$ 偏差，但无法消除非零系数下的客户端独立 $O(\gamma^2)$ 偏差，该偏差源于控制变量对局部轨迹二阶矩的间接影响及非二次曲率的转换作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的常数步长分析仅识别了 $O(\gamma/N)$ 的主导稳态均值偏差，并指出高阶偏差随客户端数量增加可能持续存在，但未能从系数层面识别出首个与客户端数量无关的偏差贡献项。

Method: 针对具有固定局部步数 $H$ 和有界加性梯度噪声的一维同质客户端全参与随机 Scaf 算法，通过数学推导证明了在 $N\ge2$ 上一致成立的稳态均值偏差展开式，分析了控制变量如何通过改变局部轨迹及其二阶矩间接产生偏差。

Result: 推导出了具体的偏差公式，显示偏差包含 $O(\gamma/N)$ 项和与 $N$ 无关的 $O(\gamma^2)$ 项；揭示了机制在于控制变量虽在线性全局平均中路径抵消，但改变了局部二阶矩，新鲜梯度噪声和持续控制波动生成的局部二阶矩修正被非二次曲率转换为稳态均值偏差；该系数在二次目标函数中为零。

Conclusion: 客户端平均不能移除系数非零时的客户端独立 $O(\gamma^2)$ 偏差分量；数值实验验证了预测系数、其随客户端数量增加的持久性及联合余项；该结果目前仅限于一维同质固定 $H$ 设定。

Abstract: Existing constant-step analysis of stochastic \Scaf{} identifies a leading $O(γ/N)$ stationary mean bias and shows that higher-order bias can persist as the client count increases, but does not identify the first client-independent contribution at coefficient level. For full-participation stochastic \Scaf{} with one-dimensional homogeneous clients, fixed local-step count $H$, and bounded additive gradient noise, we prove, uniformly over $N\ge2$,
  $$ \begin{aligned} \mathbb{E}_{π_{γ,N,H}}[x]-x^\star ={}& -\frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{4f''(x^\star)^2}\fracγ{N}\\ &- \frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{12f''(x^\star)} \frac{(H-1)(5H-1)}{H}γ^2 +O_H\!\left(\frac{γ^2}{N}+γ^3\right). \end{aligned} $$
  Hence client averaging suppresses the leading $O(γ/N)$ bias but does not remove the client-independent $O(γ^2)$ component when its coefficient is nonzero.
  The mechanism is indirect: although the direct control contribution cancels pathwise in the linear global average, the controls still alter within-round local trajectories and their second moments. Fresh gradient noise and persistent control fluctuations therefore generate local second-moment corrections that nonquadratic curvature converts into stationary mean bias. The coefficient vanishes for quadratic objectives. Numerical experiments are consistent with the predicted coefficient, its persistence as client count increases, and the stated joint remainder. The result is restricted to the one-dimensional homogeneous fixed-$H$ setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Buy-at-Bulk Facility Location on Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26337)
*Shamisa Nematollahi,Daniel Vaz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究买批量设施定位问题（BBFL）及其变体kCFL，在树结构实例上分别给出了PTAS近似算法和2-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于电信网络设计，结合了经典设施定位与买批量网络设计，旨在优化设施开设成本与满足需求的传输容量成本。

Method: 针对树实例，对单位需求和可拆分需求变体设计PTAS算法；对电缆不可拆分需求的kCFL变体，先证明星形图上近似比优于3/2的NP难性，再提出满足容量约束松弛的树算法。

Result: BBFL在树实例上存在PTAS；kCFL在电缆不可拆分需求下，星形图近似比下界为3/2，树实例上获得2-近似算法。

Conclusion: 在树拓扑结构下，BBFL及kCFL问题可通过近似算法有效求解，为电信网络中的设施布局与容量规划提供理论支持。

Abstract: We consider the buy-at-bulk facility location problem (BBFL), a problem combining the classic facility location problem with buy-at-bulk network design, which finds motivation in telecommunication networks. In it, we are given a graph with edge lengths, opening costs and demands for each vertex, and a monotone and subadditive capacity-cost function, and our task is to open facilities on a subset of the vertices and route the demand from each vertex to these facilities. The cost of a solution (which we want to minimize) is given by the opening costs of the chosen facilities, plus the cost on each edge, which is given by its length times the cost of providing enough capacity for the demands through the edge, given by the capacity-cost function. A common variant of the problem, the $k$-cable facility location problem (kCFL), considers the case where capacity is provided by buying copies of given cable types, each with a certain capacity and cost.
  We study BBFL on tree instances and show, for the unit-demand and splittable variants, that the problem admits a PTAS (a $(1+ε)$-approximation for any $ε> 0$). We also consider kCFL in the new setting of cable-unsplittable demands, where the demand of a vertex cannot be split among multiple cables. We show that the problem is NP-hard to approximate to a factor better than $3/2$ on stars, and then provide an algorithm for tree instances that outputs a solution with optimal cost, but which exceeds the capacity on each cable by a factor of $1+ε$. As a consequence, we show that the problem has a $2$-approximation algorithm on trees.

</details>


### [124] [The Time-Dependent Traveling Salesman Problem with Loose Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26360)
*Francisco J. Soulignac*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确框架来解决带时间窗的时变旅行商问题(TDTSPTW)，通过动态规划标记算法结合列生成、ng-memory增强和精确搜索，成功解决了基准测试中多达45个客户的所有实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旅行商问题带时间窗(TSPTW)方法未充分考虑交通拥堵对行驶时间的影响，需要开发能处理时变旅行时间且能在宽松时间窗下求解更大规模实例的精确方法。

Method: 开发基于动态规划标记算法的精确框架，结合列生成、ng-memory增强和精确搜索技术，使用完成界限进行状态空间稀疏化、变量固定和精确搜索剪枝，并嵌入分支定价方法中。

Result: 该框架在包含超过10,000个实例的基准测试中，成功解决了所有多达45个客户的实例，包括所有无时间窗的多达50个客户的实例，在宽松时间窗下扩展了可最优求解的实例范围。

Conclusion: 所提出的精确框架有效解决了TDTSPTW问题，在各种时间窗紧度下均保持有效性，显著提升了可最优求解的实例规模。

Abstract: The time-dependent traveling salesman problem with time windows (TDTSPTW) generalizes the well-known traveling salesman problem with time windows by accounting the effects of congestion on travel times. In this paper, we develop an exact framework for the TDTSPTW with a makespan objective that extends the range of instances solvable to optimality under loose time windows while remaining effective across all levels of time-window tightness. Our framework relies on a dynamic-programming labeling algorithm and combines column generation, ng-memory augmentation, and exact search, using completion bounds for state-space sparsification, variable fixing, and exact search pruning. Embedded within a branch-and-price method, the framework solves all instances with up to 45 customers in a benchmark comprising more than 10,000 instances, including all instances without time windows with up to 50 customers.

</details>


### [125] [Unpublished Draft: A Post-Processing Approach to Fairness in Tie-Aware Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26478)
*Somya Nigam,Johan Springael,Kenneth Sörensen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后处理方法，用于将不公平的含并列（tie-aware）共识排名转换为最接近的公平共识排名，并提供了精确算法和快速启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平排名聚合研究主要假设输入和输出均为严格排序的排列，忽略了实践中更常见的含并列（ties）排名情况，导致理论与实际应用存在差距。

Method: 提出了一种后处理框架，包含一个精确算法和一个快速启发式算法，旨在从给定的不公平含并列共识排名中计算出距离最近的公平含并列共识排名。

Result: （摘要未提供具体实验数据，但表明）成功构建了能够处理含并列情况的公平共识排名生成机制，填补了现有研究在并列排名公平性方面的空白。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入针对含并列排名的后处理方法，有效解决了现有公平排名聚合模型在实践适用性上的局限，为公平共识排名的计算提供了新的算法工具。

Abstract: The problem of finding a fair consensus ranking is an active research topic in the domain of fair rank aggregation and has been well studied; however, existing studies predominantly consider both the input rankings and the output ranking to be permutations where elements are always strictly ordered. In practice, however, a ranking with ties is far more common. This study bridges this gap by presenting a post-processing approach to determine the closest fair consensus ranking when an unfair tie-aware consensus ranking is provided. It proposes an exact algorithm and a fast heuristic to achieve this.

</details>


### [126] [Cheaper by the Batch: Shared Traversal for Genotype Graph Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26488)
*Aaron Li,Yifan Li,Drew DeHaas,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种批量突变重映射算法，通过单次共享的逆拓扑遍历替代独立的遍历，利用自适应稀疏/稠密载体集表示和位并行状态传播，在保持精确语义的前提下，将基因型表示图（GRG）的更新速度提升了最高10.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在群体遗传学的基因型表示图（GRG）中，插入或替换节点以更新突变时，现有方法需对每个突变进行独立重映射导致的计算效率低下问题，旨在在保持语义无损和复用现有结构的同时，实现可扩展的高效批量更新。

Method: 引入批量突变重映射算法，将独立的复用感知遍历替换为单次共享的逆拓扑遍历；该过程传播紧凑的位并行每突变状态，并采用适应从稀有到常见变异密度的自适应稀疏/稠密载体集表示；该方法作为基于分割的并行性的内存可扩展补充，避免了每个工作节点复制图和遍历状态。

Result: 在受控更新工作负载和端到端等位基因极化（一种常见的批量载体集更新）任务上的评估显示，该方法在保持精确载体集语义的同时，比独立重映射方法快达10.5倍。

Conclusion: 该批量重映射方法显著提升了GRG更新效率，成功解决了大规模样本下突变更新的结构编辑瓶颈，为群体遗传分析提供了高性能且内存可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Updating a graph by inserting or replacing nodes while preserving semantics and reusing existing structure is a recurring computational problem. In population genetics, this problem arises in the genotype representation graph (GRG), a directed acyclic graph that losslessly encodes phased genetic variation across hundreds of thousands of samples by sharing subgraph structure for individual mutations. In a GRG, each mutation's carrier set is implicitly encoded as the set of leaf nodes reachable from the node it is assigned to. Updating a mutation is therefore a structural editing problem, and current approaches remap mutations individually.
  This paper introduces a batched mutation-remapping algorithm that replaces independent reuse-aware traversals with a single shared reverse-topological pass, identifying reuse candidates for an entire batch at once. The pass propagates compact bit-parallel per-mutation state and uses an adaptive sparse/dense carrier set representation spanning rare-to-common variant densities. Batching is the memory-scalable complement to split-based parallelism, which instead replicates graph and traversal state per worker. Our remapping is evaluated on a controlled update workload and on end-to-end allele polarization, a bulk carrier set update that is common in population genetic analysis. Our approach is up to 10.5$\times$ faster than independent remapping while preserving exact carrier-set semantics.

</details>


### [127] [Hadamard Flattening and Gaussian Pooling Sketch for Least Squares with Coordinate-wise Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26552)
*Zhao Song,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的快速稠密随机变换算法，解决了过约束$\ell_2$回归中解向量在$\ell_\infty$范数下的坐标级精度问题，将所需的行数优化至$O(\varepsilon^{-2}d\log d)$，并修正了前人证明中关于独立性假设的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的子空间嵌入虽能保持回归成本，但难以保证解向量在$\ell_\infty$范数下接近最优解。前人（Song等）声称改进了行数至$O(\varepsilon^{-2}d\log^3 n)$，但其证明依赖一个不成立的独立性假设。本文旨在构建一个真正接近$d$线性行数的算法，并提供严谨的理论保证。

Method: 提出一种结合随机Hadamard展平、随机置换以及平衡不相交高斯池化（balanced, disjoint Gaussian pooling）的新型快速稠密随机变换。该方法通过条件独立性论证，使得在给定Hadamard和置换阶段后，草图化问题转化为噪声与整个草图化设计独立的精确高斯回归，从而弥补了前人论证的缺失。

Result: 新算法在$m=O(\varepsilon^{-2}d\log d)$行下实现了$\ell_\infty$保证；使用一次Hadamard传递，内部填充维度为$N=\widetilde{O}(n+\varepsilon^{-2}d^3)$；计算草图对$(SA, Sb)$的时间复杂度为$O(Nd\log N)=\widetilde{O}(nd+\varepsilon^{-2}d^4)$。同时，文章给出了前人工作中独立性假设失效的具体反例。

Conclusion: 通过引入新的随机变换结构并利用条件独立性，本文成功实现了过约束$\ell_2$回归中解向量的高精度$\ell_\infty$保证，且行数和计算效率均达到接近线性的优化水平，解决了现有理论中的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Randomized sketch-and-solve algorithms accelerate overconstrained $\ell_2$ regression by replacing the input with a smaller problem. Standard subspace embeddings guarantee that the cost of the regression is nearly preserved, but coordinate-wise accuracy of the solution is more delicate: we want the solution vector itself to be close to the optimal solution in $\ell_\infty$ norm. In particular, we want to find a vector $x'\in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $\|x'-x^*\|_\infty\leq \fracε{\sqrt d}\cdot \|Ax^\star-b\|_2\cdot \|A^\dagger\|_{\rm op}$. Price, Song and Woodruff initiated the study of this problem and showed that the subsampled randomized Hadamard transform (SRHT) with $O(ε^{-2} d^{1+Θ(\sqrt{\log\log n/\log d})})$ rows achieves this guarantee. A subsequent work of Song, Ye, Yin and Zhang claimed to improve the row count to $O(ε^{-2}d\log^3 n)$. Unfortunately, their proof relies on an independence assumption that does not hold in general, and we exhibit an explicit instance on which it fails.
  To achieve a truly nearly-linear-in-$d$ row count, we introduce a new fast, dense randomized transform, which combines a randomized Hadamard flattening, a random permutation, and balanced, disjoint Gaussian pooling. Conditioned on the Hadamard-and-permutation stage, the sketched problem becomes an exact Gaussian regression in which the noise is independent of the entire sketched design; this conditional independence is exactly what the earlier argument was missing. Our sketch yields the $\ell_\infty$ guarantee with $m=O(ε^{-2}d\log d)$ rows, uses one Hadamard pass with a padded internal dimension $N=\widetilde{O}(n+ε^{-2}d^3)$, and is efficient to apply: the sketched pair $(SA, Sb)$ can be computed in $O(Nd\log N)=\widetilde{O}(nd+ε^{-2}d^4)$ time.

</details>


### [128] [On the Instance Optimality of Bidirectional Dijkstra's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26952)
*Matic Požar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文修正了先前关于Dijkstra算法实例最优性分析的缺陷，提出了一种改进的双向Dijkstra变体，证明了其在加权图中的实例最优性，并针对无权图和简单图场景建立了新的下界及最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Haeupler等人关于最短路径算法实例最优性工作的启发，作者旨在重新审视标准查询模型下最短$st$-path算法的实例最优性问题，特别是针对先前分析中存在的缺陷和反例进行修正与完善。

Method: 1. 识别单向和双向Dijkstra算法实例最优性分析中的问题并构造反例；2. 提出双向Dijkstra算法的最小简化修改方案；3. 简化无权图情况下的下界证明；4. 针对简单图设定，基于节点数$n$、查询边数$m$及最大度$\Delta$的关系推导最优性条件。

Result: 1. 证明了改进后的双向Dijkstra变体在加权设置下具有实例最优性；2. 确认无权图中不存在优于$O(\Delta)$因子的实例最优算法；3. 在简单图中，若满足$n \ge m/16$或最大度不超过探索边数的平方根，算法在常数因子内达到最优。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了双向Dijkstra算法在加权图中的实例最优性问题，澄清了无权图的理论下界，并在简单图的特定条件下取得了实例最优性的新进展，为近似算法的设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Recent work by Haeupler, Hladík, Rozhon, Tarjan, and Tětek on the instance optimality of shortest-path algorithms established several results concerning Dijkstra's algorithm and bidirectional Dijkstra's algorithm in weighted and unweighted graphs. Motivated by these results, we revisit the question of instance optimality for shortest $st$-path algorithms in the standard query model.
  We identify several issues in the analysis of the instance optimality of both unidirectional and bidirectional Dijkstra's algorithms and provide corresponding counterexamples. We then propose a minimal simple modification of the bidirectional Dijkstra algorithm and prove that the resulting variant is instance optimal in the weighted setting. Furthermore, we revisit the unweighted case, provide a simplified proof of the lower bound showing that no algorithm can achieve instance optimality up to a factor better than $O(Δ)$, where $Δ$ denotes the maximum degree of the graph, and discuss the implications of this result for approximation algorithms. Finally, we make progress on the open problem of instance optimality in simple graphs. We show that if the problem instance satisfies $n\ge m/16$, where $n$ is the number of nodes and $m$ is the number of edges queried by our algorithm, then it is optimal up to a constant factor. Additionally, we show instance optimality for a broad class of instances, in particular when the largest degree in the graph is at most the square root of the number of explored edges, our algorithm exhibits optimality up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [129] [The Randomized Query Complexity of Finding Minimal Elements in Bounded-Width Posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26981)
*Luyao Fan,Jiayang Zou,Jiayang Gao,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在宽度为 $w$ 的 $n$ 元素偏序集中查找所有极小元素的零错误随机查询复杂度的下界，填补了此前研究与上界之间的常数因子差距，确认了已知上界渐近领先常数的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Daskalakis 等人的工作虽然建立了 $\frac{w+1}{2}n$ 的随机上界，但对应的下界存在乘法间隙，且随着 $w$ 增大该间隙趋近于因子 2。本研究旨在消除这一间隙，确定精确的渐近领先常数。

Method: 基于随机链硬分布（random-chain hard distribution），采用对不可比查询的成对计数方法（pairwise accounting），并利用分量翻转对合（component-flip involution）及不可比比较的唯一所有权性质（unique ownership property）进行论证。

Result: 证明了有限下界公式 $R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w}\ge \frac{w+1}{2}n-\frac{w(w+3)}4 +w\left(1-\frac1w\right)^n +\frac{w(w-1)}4\left(1-\frac2w\right)^n$，并得出对于任意固定 $w$，复杂度为 $\left(\frac{w+1}{2}+o(1)\right)n$。

Conclusion: 已知的随机上界具有正确的渐近领先常数，彻底解决了该问题在固定宽度下的零错误随机查询复杂度的渐近行为问题。

Abstract: We study the zero-error randomized query complexity of finding all minimal elements in an unknown $n$-element poset of width at most $w$. Previous work of Daskalakis, Karp, Mossel, Riesenfeld, and Verbin established a randomized upper bound with leading term $\frac{w+1}{2}n$, while the corresponding lower bound left a multiplicative gap in the leading constant that approaches a factor of 2 as $w$ grows. We prove the finite lower bound \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w}\ge \frac{w+1}{2}n-\frac{w(w+3)}4 +w\left(1-\frac1w\right)^n +\frac{w(w-1)}4\left(1-\frac2w\right)^n. \) Consequently, for every fixed $w$, \( R^{\mathrm{LV}}_{n,w} =
\left(\frac{w+1}{2}+o(1)\right)n. \) Thus the known randomized upper bound has the correct asymptotic leading constant for every fixed width. The argument is based on a pairwise accounting of incomparable queries under a random-chain hard distribution, using a component-flip involution and a unique ownership property for incomparable comparisons. Generative AI was used in the preparation of this manuscript.

</details>


### [130] [Product Structure Meets Track Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27096)
*Michael A. Bekos,Giordano Da Lozzo,Petr Hliněný,Michael Kaufmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算法，用于计算具有乘积结构图的轨道布局（track layout）上界，该算法为平面图、1-平面图及多种k-相关图类建立了新的或匹配的轨道数上界，且运行时间为线性或接近线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定图的轨道数（即图允许轨道布局所需的最小轨道数），特别是针对具有特定乘积结构（product structure）的图类，以解决现有文献中部分图类轨道数上界缺失或不优的问题。

Method: 提出一种新算法，针对强乘积 $P^h \boxtimes K_r \boxtimes H$ 的每个子图，计算其轨道布局。该算法利用初等链表数据结构，所需轨道数上限为 $(2h+1) \cdot r \cdot tn(H)$，并结合现有的乘积结构分解结果应用于各类图。

Result: 1. 平面图的轨道数上界匹配当前最佳值225；
2. 1-平面图和最优2-平面图的轨道数上界为375；
3. 为 genus-k、k-平面、k-框架、k-映射及k-串图建立了仅依赖于k的新上界；
4. 算法在平面图上呈线性时间运行，一般情况下的时间复杂度为 $O(n + h \cdot r \cdot t + f_t(H))$。

Conclusion: 该研究通过利用图的乘积结构特性，成功推导出了多种重要图类的轨道数上界，并提供了一种高效且仅需基础数据结构的构造算法，显著推进了图轨道布局领域的理论边界。

Abstract: A track layout of a graph is a partition of its vertices into linearly ordered independent sets, called tracks, such that no two edges between the same pair of tracks cross. Given a graph, the goal in this context is to determine its track number, that is, the minimum number of tracks required for the graph to admit a track layout.
  In this work, we present upper bounds on the track number of graphs admitting a product structure. Our main contribution is an algorithm that computes a track layout with at most $(2h+1) \cdot r \cdot tn(H)$ tracks for every subgraph of the strong product $P^h \boxtimes K_r \boxtimes H$, where $P^h$ is the $h$-th power of a path $P$, $K_r$ is the complete graph on $r$ vertices, and $H$ is a graph with track number $tn(H)$. Combined with existing product-structure results from the literature, this algorithm yields upper bounds on the track number of several graph classes. For planar graphs, the obtained bound matches the current best-known upper bound of $225$. For $1$-planar and optimal $2$-planar graphs, our algorithm yields track layouts with at most $375$ tracks, while for genus-$k$, $k$-planar, $k$-framed, $k$-map, and $k$-string graphs it provides track layouts with a number of tracks that depends solely on $k$, thus establishing new upper bounds on the track number for these graph classes. The algorithm runs in linear time for planar graphs and, more generally, in $O(n + h \cdot r \cdot t + f_t(H))$ time whenever a corresponding product-structure decomposition of the input $n$-vertex graph is provided as part of the input, where $t=tn(H)$ and $f_t(H)$ is the time needed to compute a $t$-track layout of $H$. Furthermore, our algorithm only uses elementary linked-list data structures.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Weight Distributions of Single Parity-Check Product Codes via Character Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26457)
*Makson Miller Alves Ribeiro,Sara D. Cardell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元单奇偶校验乘积码的结构与枚举性质，确定了其广义汉明重量层次、最大码字重量及重量枚举器，并推导了无需穷举即可计算完整重量分布的精确闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入理解二元单奇偶校验乘积码（SPC product codes）的代数结构，特别是解决其重量分布（weight distribution）和广义汉明重量（generalized Hamming weights）的精确计算问题，以避免低效的穷举枚举。

Method: 1. 刻画对偶码结构；2. 应用Walsh-Hadamard形式的MacWilliams恒等式推导重量枚举器的闭式表达；3. 按汉明重量分组辅助二元向量，利用二项式系数和交替卷积表示系数；4. 引入Krawtchouk多项式构建计算完整重量分布的精确算法。

Result: 确定了乘积码$\mathcal{C}_{m,n}$的广义汉明重量层次；证明了方阵乘积码$\mathcal{C}_n$的最大码字重量，并证得其齐次重量枚举器对称当且仅当$n$为偶数；导出了重量枚举器系数的显式公式及基于Krawtchouk多项式的非穷举计算过程。

Conclusion: 通过理论推导与数值算例验证，本文成功建立了计算二元单奇偶校验乘积码重量分布的精确解析方法，揭示了其重量枚举器的对称性条件，为该类码的性能分析提供了有效的数学工具。

Abstract: We investigate structural and enumerative properties of binary single parity-check product codes. For each $n\geq 2$, $\operatorname{SPC}(n)$ denotes the binary single parity-check code of length $n$, consisting of all binary vectors of length $n$ having even Hamming weight. We determine the generalized Hamming weight hierarchy of the product code $\mathcal{C}_{m,n}=\operatorname{SPC}(m)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, whose codewords can be represented as $m\times n$ binary matrices in which every row and every column has even Hamming weight. For the square product $\mathcal{C}_n =\operatorname{SPC}(n)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, we also determine the maximum codeword weight and prove that its homogeneous weight enumerator is symmetric if and only if $n$ is even. After characterizing the dual code, we apply the MacWilliams identity in its Walsh--Hadamard formulation to derive an exact closed-form expression for the weight enumerator. By grouping the auxiliary binary vectors according to their Hamming weights, we obtain an explicit formula for each coefficient in terms of binomial coefficients and alternating convolutions. Finally, using Krawtchouk polynomials, we present an exact procedure for computing the full weight distribution without exhaustively enumerating all codewords. Numerical examples illustrate the formulas and verify the resulting computations.

</details>


### [132] [Knowledge Distillation Driven Semantic NOMA with GAN Refinement for 6G Robotic Vehicle Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27198)
*Qifei Wang,Zhen Gao,Li Qiao,Ziwei Wan,De Mi,Dapeng Li,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出KDG-SemNOMA框架，通过知识蒸馏和生成模型增强NOMA通信，实现6G机器人车辆在高保真视觉感知下的高效、绿色通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G赋能的机器人车辆需要高保真视觉感知，但面临带宽和能量限制。语义通信虽高效，但在上行NOMA网络中受严重干扰影响。

Method: 1. 开发基于ConvNeXt的DeepJSCC架构，带增强注意力特征模块用于动态信道适应；2. 采用两阶段知识蒸馏策略，由正交传输教师模型指导NOMA学生模型以减轻干扰；3. 引入通道条件GAN(cGAN)解决像素级优化导致的过平滑问题，提升图像纹理真实感。

Result: 在FFHQ-256数据集上的实验表明，KDG-SemNOMA在像素级精度和感知保真度方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提KDG-SemNOMA框架有效解决了6G机器人车辆通信中的干扰和图像质量退化问题，实现了鲁棒且绿色的语义通信。

Abstract: To achieve sustainable intelligent mobility, 6G-empowered robotic vehicles (RVs) require high-fidelity visual perception under stringent bandwidth and energy constraints. Semantic communication offers a spectral-efficient solution but suffers from severe interference in uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) RV networks. To address this, we propose a knowledge distillation-driven and generative models-enhanced NOMA framework for robust and green RV communications, named KDG-SemNOMA. First, we develop a ConvNeXt-based deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) architecture with an enhanced attention feature (AF) module for dynamic channel adaptation. Second, to mitigate interference without inference overhead, an orthogonal transmission teacher model guides the NOMA student model via a two-stage knowledge distillation strategy. Finally, to address the over-smoothing artifacts of pixel-wise optimization, we introduce a channel-conditional GAN (cGAN). By explicitly taking the Stage-I initial reconstruction and channel states as conditional inputs, this module refines coarse outputs into high-fidelity images with realistic textures. Experiments on FFHQ-256 demonstrate that KDG-SemNOMA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both pixel-level accuracy and perceptual fidelity.

</details>


### [133] [Decoding Algorithms for MDS Array Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26483)
*Sara D. Cardell,Gustavo Terra Bastos,Cintya Wink de Oliveira Benedito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于超正则矩阵与非奇异矩阵克罗内克积构造的MDS阵列码的解码过程，针对擦除信道和q元对称信道分别提出了高效的解码算法，并特别探讨了当超正则矩阵为范德蒙德矩阵时的代数结构优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用该类MDS阵列码校验矩阵的特殊结构，开发适用于不同信道模型（擦除信道和q元对称信道）的高效解码算法，以解决特定错误模式下的符号恢复问题。

Method: 通过分析校验矩阵的克罗内克积结构，针对擦除信道设计可恢复任意$n-k$个符号擦除的算法；针对q元对称信道，推导单符号和双符号错误的定位与数值计算的显式过程；并利用范德蒙德矩阵的额外代数特性优化解码流程。

Result: 成功构建了针对擦除信道的通用恢复算法，以及针对q元对称信道中单/双符号错误的显式解码程序；通过不同有限域上的具体实例验证了所提过程的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的解码算法充分利用了MDS阵列码的代数结构，能够有效处理擦除和少量符号错误，特别是在采用范德蒙德矩阵构造时，进一步提升了解码的可行性和效率。

Abstract: We study decoding procedures for a family of MDS array codes previously constructed from the Kronecker product of a superregular matrix and a non-singular matrix over a finite field. By exploiting the particular structure of their parity-check matrices, we develop decoding algorithms for different channel models. For the erasure channel, we provide an algorithm capable of recovering any pattern of up to $n-k$ symbol erasures. For the $q$-ary symmetric channel, we investigate the decoding of one and two symbol errors and give explicit procedures for determining their locations and values. We also consider the particular case in which the superregular matrix is a Vandermonde matrix, showing how its additional algebraic structure can be exploited in the decoding process. Explicit examples over different finite fields are provided to illustrate the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [134] [Sharp Minimax Regret for Infinite-Memory Logistic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26515)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有无限输入记忆的有限字母外源驱动源的在线预测问题，通过局部贝叶斯混合和Toeplitz设计逆定理证明了滞后解析谱Γ_T(r)是该源类在典型机制下的最小最大累积遗憾尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有无限记忆的外源驱动源的在线预测问题，理解滞后对预测的影响以及累积遗憾的理论界限。

Method: 使用局部贝叶斯混合证明上界，通过Toeplitz设计逆定理证明下界，并分析截断输入对遗憾的影响。

Result: 证明了Γ_T(r)是累积遗憾的最小最大尺度，对于指数和多项式包络分别得到Θ(α^{-1}\log^2T)和Θ(T^{1/(2s)})的遗憾界，并提出了达到谱上界的缩放在线牛顿预测器。

Conclusion: Γ_T(r)确定了该类源的最小最大累积遗憾尺度，截断最近h个输入的成本与最坏情况截断配置文件可能导致多项式不同的遗憾。

Abstract: We study online prediction for a specific finite-alphabet, exogenously driven source with infinite input memory. Independent Rademacher inputs $(U_t)$ are observed sequentially, and the next binary mark has logit $\sum_{j=1}^{t}θ_jU_{t+1-j}$, where $\abs{θ_j}\leq r_j$ and $\sum_jr_j\leq B$. Regret is expected cumulative excess log loss. Lag $j$ can affect prediction by scale $r_j$ and enters only $n_{T,j}=T-j+1$ prediction rounds, leading to the lag-resolved spectrum $Γ_T(r)=\sum_{j=1}^{T}\log\!\left(1+n_{T,j}r_j^2\right)$. For every summable envelope, a localized Bayesian mixture proves $\cR_T(r)\leq CΓ_T(r)$. For exponential and polynomial envelopes, under the stated finite-sample dimension condition, a Toeplitz-design converse proves $\cR_T(r)\geq cΓ_T(r)$, with constants allowed to depend on the fixed decay parameters and the logit bound. Thus $Γ_T(r)$ is the minimax cumulative-regret scale for this source class in these canonical regimes, giving $Θ(α^{-1}\log^2T)$ for $r_j=Ae^{-αj}$ and $Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$ for $r_j=Aj^{-s}$, $s>1$. The converse is specific to the exogenous lagged model and is not a profile-only theorem for arbitrary stationary infinite-memory sources. Retaining only the most recent $h$ inputs costs order $\sum_{j>h}n_{T,j}θ_j^2$, yet the same worst-case truncation profile can correspond to polynomially different regret. A scaled online Newton predictor attains the spectrum upper bound.

</details>


### [135] [Spectral Approximation and Ergodic-Capacity Convergence of HMIMO Channels under Spatial-Wavenumber Domain Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26802)
*Hangsong Yan,Hong Yang,Shu Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有方形孔径和物理规定圆形波数支持的连续全息多输入多输出（HMIMO）信道的有限维谱近似和遍历容量收敛性，提出了一种基于张量积子空间的投影方法，并证明了谱近似误差和容量差距具有超指数衰减特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决连续 HMIMO 信道中因空间 - 波数域不匹配导致的非可分离方盘集中问题，经典基于 PSWF 的可分离构造无法直接应用，需要建立有效的有限维近似方法以准确评估信道性能。

Method: 将连续算子投影到一维 PSWF 的张量积子空间上，同时保持圆形波数支持，生成非对角但高度稀疏的有限维矩阵；开发具有明确径向和角节点阈值的求积规则来评估投影矩阵。

Result: 证明了全谱近似误差受一维 PSWF 特征值尾包络控制，在截断阈值外超指数衰减；建立了连续信道与截断信道遍历容量差距的非渐近上界，该界限同样具有超指数依赖性；数值结果显示传统截断方法可能遗漏性能相关模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的张量 PSWF 截断方法能有效处理非可分离问题，提供精确的谱近似和容量收敛保证，优于传统基于空间自由度的截断方法，特别适用于紧凑孔径场景。

Abstract: We establish quantitative results on finite-dimensional spectral approximation and ergodic-capacity convergence for continuous Holographic Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (HMIMO) channels with square apertures and physically prescribed circular wavenumber support. The resulting spatial-wavenumber domain mismatch leads to a non-separable square-disk concentration problem for which the classical separable construction based on prolate spheroidal wave functions (PSWFs) cannot be directly applied. We project the continuous operator onto a tensor-product subspace of one-dimensional (1D) PSWFs while preserving the circular wavenumber support, yielding a generally non-diagonal but highly sparse finite-dimensional matrix. We show that the whole-spectrum approximation error, accounting for retained-eigenvalue perturbations and the residual spectral tail, remains controlled by a 1D PSWF eigenvalue-tail envelope despite the loss of separability and induced off-diagonal coupling. Beyond an explicit 1D truncation threshold, this error decays super-exponentially. This analysis further yields an asymptotic upper envelope for the eigenspectrum under the flattened two-dimensional eigenvalue ordering. We further establish a non-asymptotic upper bound on the gap between the actual ergodic capacities of the continuous and tensor-PSWF-truncated channels under their respective transmit-covariance optimizations. Combined with the spectral result, this capacity-gap bound inherits the same super-exponential dependence on the truncation order. Finally, quadrature rules with explicit radial and angular node thresholds are developed for evaluating the projected matrix. Numerical results show that conventional truncation based on spatial degrees of freedom can omit performance-relevant modes, particularly for compact apertures.

</details>


### [136] [Minimum Rate For Partially Observable Linear System with Side Information: LQG Plant and Gaussian-Markov Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26917)
*Sijie Li,Hyeji Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有边信息的部分可观测线性系统所需的最小速率，证明了线性策略类足以优化条件定向信息下界，且标量情况下的优化问题是凸的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注完全或部分观测情况，或完全观测加边信息的情况，缺乏对部分可观测系统结合边信息的最小速率研究的统一框架。

Method: 考虑LQG系统和Gaussian-Markov源，分析线性策略类对条件定向信息下界的优化能力，并研究标量情况下优化问题的凸性。

Result: 证明了线性策略类足以优化条件定向信息下界，标量情况下优化问题在时变和时不变系统中均为凸问题，推广了以往仅考虑完全或部分观测的研究。

Conclusion: 边信息对部分可观测系统的最小速率有显著影响，数值模拟验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum rate required for a partially observable linear system with side information. The Linear Quadratic Gaussian(LQG) plant and the Gaussian-Markov source are considered. We show that a class of linear policies is sufficient for optimizing the conditional directed information lower bound. We also show that the resulting optimization problem is convex for the scalar case in both time-varying and time-invariant systems. Our results generalize the past works that consider the case with full or partial observation only, and the case with full observation and side information. Numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the effect of side information for partially observable systems.

</details>


### [137] [Real-Time Reconstruction of Markov Sources over MPR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27116)
*Pansee S. Elessawy,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多包接收（MPR）共享无线信道下，两个二元马尔可夫源的实时重建与远程驱动问题，通过优化采样策略最小化加权实时重建误差（RTE）或驱动误差（CAE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于碰撞模型，会丢弃同时传输的数据包，而本文利用MPR技术，通过重建误差和驱动误差而非原始传输率来评估通信性能，旨在找到满足采样约束的最优采样策略。

Method: 首先推导RTE和CAE关于有效更新概率的闭式表达式；针对传感器独立随机化导致的非凸优化问题，利用可达更新率区域的几何特性，将帕累托最优策略搜索简化为有限的一维边界分支搜索；以时间共享联合传感器动作为基准，证明最多两个帕累托极端模式即可，并量化独立随机化带来的损失。

Result: 获得了RTE和CAE的闭式表达式；证明了独立随机化策略下最优搜索可简化为有限的一维边界分支搜索；证明了时间共享基准下最多只需两个帕累托极端模式；数值结果表明仅具备MPR能力不足够，只有当两个源的同时接收都可靠时，同时解码才能提升任务导向目标。

Conclusion: MPR能力本身并不足以保证性能提升，同时解码仅在双源并发接收可靠时有效，否则避免同时传输的策略可能同样有效，为任务导向的通信系统设计提供了理论依据和优化方法。

Abstract: This paper studies the real-time reconstruction and remote actuation of two binary Markov sources over a shared wireless channel with multi-packet reception (MPR). Unlike many existing collision-based formulations that discard simulta- neous transmissions, we exploit MPR and evaluate communica- tion through reconstruction and actuation errors rather than raw delivery rates. We consider two sensors observing the sources and aim to find sampling policies that minimize the weighted real- time reconstruction error (RTE), or equivalently the weighted cost of actuation error (CAE) for the considered binary sources, under per-sensor sampling constraints. We first obtain closed- form expressions for the RTE and CAE in terms of the effective update probabilities. When the sensors randomize independently, the MPR-induced update-rate map becomes bilinear, and the constrained optimization is nonconvex. We exploit the geometry of the achievable update-rate region to show that the search over Pareto-efficient independently randomized policies reduces to a finite set of one-dimensional boundary-branch searches with closed-form candidates. As a benchmark, we allow time sharing among joint sensor actions, and we prove that at most two Pareto- extreme modes are sufficient, and use this benchmark to quantify the loss caused by independent randomization. Numerical results reveal that MPR capability alone is not sufficient; simultaneous decoding improves the task-oriented objective only when con- current reception is reliable for both sources; otherwise, policies that avoid simultaneous transmissions can be equally effective.

</details>


### [138] [Deterministic Identification over Additive Gaussian Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27243)
*Jonathan E. W. Huffmann,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对加性高斯信道（特别是加性白高斯信道）中确定性识别容量未知的问题，引入格论（lattice theory）新视角，建立了该容量的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代通信系统对数据率、可靠性和能效有严格要求，基于信道的识别范式能提供比传统信道编码更高的识别率。确定性识别因在实现复杂度与通信增益间取得平衡，成为未来系统（如分子通信）的关键方案。然而，即便是最简单的加性白高斯信道，其确定性识别容量至今未知，亟需理论突破。

Method: 引入确定性识别的新视角，并应用格论（lattice theory）的相关结果进行推导。

Result: 成功建立了加性高斯信道确定性识别容量的紧界（tight bounds）。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了后香农信息论中加性高斯信道确定性识别容量的理论空白，为未来高能效通信系统的设计提供了重要的理论依据。

Abstract: Modern communication systems impose strict demands on data rate,
  reliability, and power efficiency. In this context, emerging communication
  paradigms such as identification via channels have become an important topic
  in post-Shannon information theory, offering the potential for substantially
  higher identification rates than in conventional channel coding.Deterministic
  identification is particularly interesting for specialized communication
  scenarios because it provides a balance between implementation complexity and
  the communication gains due to higher identification rates. It is therefore a
  promising communication scheme for future communication systems, including
  molecular communication systems.
  Additive Gaussian channels, particularly the additive white Gaussian channel,
  are among the most important channel models for analyzing the performance of
  communication systems in information and communication theory. This importance
  stems from both their mathematical tractability and their ubiquitous
  appearance in practical applications.
  To date the deterministic identification capacity for additive Gaussian
  channels remains unknown even for the simplest case of the additive white
  Gaussian channel. In this paper, we establish tight bounds on the
  deterministic identification capacity of additive Gaussian channels by
  introducing a new perspective on deterministic identification. To this end, we
  apply results from lattice theory to obtain new capacity results.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [TRACE: Retrospective Streaming Generation of Physical Fields under Sparse Structured Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26219)
*Xinyu Zhang,Lihao Chen,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种用于结构化传感下物理场重建的回顾性流式生成框架，通过潜在空间贝叶斯推断和卡尔曼滤波融合时序先验，在稀疏观测下实现高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式重建方法多假设固定批量条件输入，但实际传感系统常产生结构化流式数据（如局部扫描、移动视场、帧缺失），缺乏针对此类场景的有效重建框架。

Method: TRACE在连续坐标潜在空间中进行近似贝叶斯推断，将稀疏离格测量转化为生成潜在证据，通过类卡尔曼滤波融合状态空间时序先验，并利用回顾性平滑优化观测不足的过往帧。

Result: 在活性物质、海洋声速场和超新星模拟实验中，TRACE在时间和空间稀疏传感协议下，重建质量匹配或超越帧式生成重建器、离线时空方法及流式数据同化基线。

Conclusion: TRACE为结构化传感下的物理场流式重建提供了有效解决方案，显著提升了稀疏观测场景中的重建性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is central to scientific monitoring, inverse modeling, and digital-twin construction. Generative reconstruction has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for this task by learning data-driven physical priors that complete plausible full fields from limited observations. However, existing methods largely assume fixed, batch conditioning, whereas real sensing systems often produce structured streams: probes scan local regions, instruments observe moving fields of view, and communication constraints may leave entire frames missing. We propose TRACE, a retrospective streaming generative reconstruction framework for physical fields under structured sensing. TRACE performs approximate Bayesian inference in a learned continuous-coordinate latent space, converting sparse off-grid measurements into generative latent evidence, fusing it with a state-space temporal prior through Kalman-style filtering, and refining under-observed past frames via retrospective smoothing. Experiments on active matter, ocean sound-speed fields, and supernova simulations show that TRACE matches or surpasses frame-wise generative reconstructors, offline spatiotemporal methods, and streaming data-assimilation baselines in reconstruction quality under temporally sparse and spatially localized sensing protocols.

</details>


### [140] [A Unified Descriptive-Complexity Framework for Model Selection under Correlated Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26618)
*Yanhang Zhang,Wei Liu,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种描述性复杂性信息准则（DCIC），用于在强预测变量依赖和模型类别不确定性下解决模型选择问题，无需依赖 RIP 类条件即可实现选择一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在指数级数量的模型、强预测变量依赖以及模型类别不确定性的情况下，传统的模型选择方法面临挑战，需要一种能够在不依赖严格假设（如 RIP 条件）的情况下实现选择一致性并提供非渐近 Oracle 风险界的方法。

Method: 提出描述性复杂性信息准则（DCIC），通过 Kraft 可容许码长对大型候选模型集合进行正则化，在次 Weibull 噪声下建立选择一致性，并开发复杂性引导的搜索路径以明确计算与统计的权衡。

Result: 在模型误设下仍有效的非渐近 Oracle 风险界，通过适当的可识别性条件实现类别 - 模型恢复和跨类别的风险适应，大惩罚值以高概率产生多项式大小的保留搜索区域。

Conclusion: DCIC 方法在强依赖和模型类别不确定性下表现出稳定的支持恢复和有利的估计性能，为模型选择提供了一种有效的复杂性正则化框架。

Abstract: Model selection becomes particularly challenging under strong predictor dependence and model-class uncertainty, especially when there are exponentially many models. We propose a Descriptive-Complexity Information Criterion (DCIC) that regularizes large candidate model collections through Kraft-admissible code lengths. Under sub-Weibull noise, we establish selection consistency through approximation-error separation without relying on RIP-type conditions, together with nonasymptotic oracle risk bounds that remain valid under model misspecification. The same coding principle places heterogeneous classes on a common complexity scale at a small additional class-identification cost. This extension yields class--model recovery under suitable identifiability conditions and risk adaptation across classes. We further develop a complexity-guided search path that makes the computation--statistics trade-off explicit. Large penalties yield polynomial-size retained search regions with high probability, whereas smaller penalties sharpen the oracle risk benchmark. Numerical experiments illustrate stable support recovery and favorable estimation performance under strong dependence and model-class uncertainty.

</details>


### [141] [Incremental Recommendation via Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26804)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,David Gustafsson,Michael O'Riordan,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果架构的推荐策略，利用现有的留置（holdback）实验数据，通过双阈值目标策略解决归因窗口不匹配问题，在减少7%推荐曝光量的同时未显著降低内容消费量，并证明了因果模型能学习更具泛化性的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐曝光量是有限资源，向那些本就会自然发现内容的用户进行推荐不会产生增量价值，反而挤占了其他潜在推荐的资源。现有的生产模型缺乏因果推断能力，无法有效区分“自然发现”与“推荐驱动”的用户行为，导致资源浪费。

Method: 扩展现有的生产推荐模型至因果架构，利用常规实验基础设施中已收集的留置数据进行训练，无需新数据采集。针对处理组（短直接响应窗口）与留置组（多日自然窗口）之间归因窗口不匹配导致朴素处理效应减法无效的问题，提出双阈值目标策略：仅在“处理流概率高”且“自然流概率低”时交付推荐。

Result: 在涉及数百万Spotify用户的大规模生产级A/B测试中，该策略将推荐曝光量减少了7%，且整体推荐内容消费量无统计学意义上的显著下降。此外，联合训练留置数据提高了处理头（treated head）相对于生产基线的校准度。

Conclusion: 该双阈值策略有效优化了推荐资源的分配效率。联合训练带来的校准度提升表明，相较于仅使用观测数据训练的模型，因果模型能够学习到更具泛化性的表征。

Abstract: Recommendation impressions are a finite resource, hence delivering a recommendation to a user who would discover the content organically yields no incremental value and displaces other recommendations that could. We address this by extending an existing production recommendation model to a causal architecture using holdback data that is already collected as part of routine experimentation infrastructure, requiring no new data collection. A central challenge is that attribution windows differ between treated and holdback observations: treated users are attributed a stream within a short direct-response window, while holdback users are attributed organic streams over a multi-day window. This mismatch makes naive treatment-effect subtraction invalid. We resolve this with a dual-threshold targeting policy that delivers a recommendation only when the probability of a treated stream is high and the probability of organic stream is low. In a production-scale A/B test on millions of Spotify users, this policy reduces recommendation impressions by 7% with no statistically significant reduction in overall recommended content consumption. We further show that joint training with holdback data improves calibration of the treated head relative to the production baseline, and argue this can be taken as evidence that causal models learn more generalisable representations than models trained on observational data alone.

</details>


### [142] [Why not to use the Gaussian kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26974)
*Toni Karvonen,Chris J. Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 文章认为高斯核在回归和分类任务中应该避免使用，不应作为默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核在 Gaussian 过程回归中非常流行，但作者认为其存在严重问题，需要重新评估其适用性。

Method: 通过分析高斯核的条件方差特性和数值条件性，论证其过于平滑导致的分析性问题。

Result: 高斯核导致条件方差过小，造成预测不确定性被严重低估；同时导致数值病态，需要额外技巧如 nugget 项来修正模型。

Conclusion: 应避免使用高斯核，更广泛地说，应避免使用分析性核函数，因为其在谱密度上表现为指数衰减，导致不自然的平滑性。

Abstract: Kernels measure similarity or correlation in tasks such as regression and classification. The Gaussian kernel, other names of which include squared exponential and radial basis function kernel, is one of the most popular in Gaussian process regression. We argue that the Gaussian kernel is best avoided and should never be used as a default. The argument rests on two results demonstrating that the Gaussian kernel is extremely brittle. First, the Gaussian kernel gives rise to a conditional variance that is unrealistically small. If the variance is used to quantify predictive uncertainty, catastrophic overconfidence is almost inevitable. Second, a small variance goes hand in hand with numerical ill-conditioning, so that to use the Gaussian kernel in practice requires tricks such as nugget terms that effectively modify the underlying regression or classification model. These problems are caused by the unnatural smoothness of the Gaussian kernel, a fact we are far from the first to take notice of. The problem is not the Gaussian form itself but the analyticity of the kernel: Our argument is more broadly that analytic kernels are best avoided. For stationary kernels analyticity is essentially equivalent to an exponential decay of the spectral density.

</details>


### [143] [Representation Measurements Under Function-Preserving Reparameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27020)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型中隐藏坐标的表示派生测量应保持不变，但列置换并行分析违反了这一原则，导致组件计数和决策不稳定，而正交不变比较器分数则保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的隐藏坐标不由输入输出函数唯一确定，因此表示派生的测量应对于保持函数不变的基变换具有不变性。然而，现有的列置换并行分析方法可能违反这种不变性，导致测量结果依赖于隐藏坐标的选择而非模型本身的属性。

Method: 研究通过在五个模型、三个检索领域和75种变换上进行实证分析，比较了列置换并行分析与正交不变比较器分数的表现，并使用仅中心化控制来隔离参考驱动效应。

Result: 列置换并行分析在中位组件计数分歧为0.79，中位固定阈值决策分歧为0.26。在1200个组件计数中，有1141个发生变化，而独立并行分析种子未改变任何对应决策。正交不变比较器分数在数值上保持稳定，且具有相似的保留判别能力。

Conclusion: 并行分析得出的组件计数和决策可能反映隐藏坐标的选择，而非模型的明确定义属性。因此，应使用正交不变的比较器分数来确保测量结果的稳定性和可靠性。

Abstract: Hidden coordinates are not uniquely determined by a language model's input--output function, so representation-derived measurements should be invariant to function-preserving changes of basis. This study shows that column-permutation parallel analysis violates function-preserving reparameterization invariance because its reference distribution and selected component count can change while the model function and observed covariance spectrum remain fixed. More generally, a data-internal reference procedure cannot simultaneously preserve every coordinate marginal, remain orthogonally equivariant, and remove cross-coordinate covariance. Empirically, across five models, three retrieval domains, and 75 transformations, median component-count disagreement is 0.79 and median fixed-threshold decision disagreement is 0.26. A centering-only control isolates the reference-driven effect, with 1,141 of 1,200 component counts changing despite an unchanged observed spectrum, whereas independent parallel analysis seeds change none of the corresponding decisions. By contrast, orthogonally invariant comparator scores remain numerically stable with similar held-out discrimination. Together, these results show that parallel analysis-derived component counts and decisions can reflect hidden-coordinate choice rather than a well-defined property of the model.

</details>


### [144] [Active Diffusion-Based Inference for Ill-Posed Inverse Problems under Incomplete Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27080)
*Jitao Xu,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主动扩散的反问题求解器，通过迭代检测和纠正模型错误，即使在初始训练边界排除真实参数的情况下，也能发现并学习参数空间的正确区域，适用于科学和工程中的反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程应用中，从实验可观测数据估计未知参数是一个反问题，由于非线性、噪声和不适定性而具有挑战性，需要一种鲁棒的求解方法。

Method: 训练扩散模型学习参数空间与可观测空间之间的映射，通过后验不确定性迭代检测和纠正模型错误，实现自适应域增强。

Result: 在具有无限解的玩具反问题和量子色动力学分析中核子结构的事件可观测量量子相关函数参数化中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为自适应域增强提供了原则性的贝叶斯 justification，确保在不完全先验知识下反问题的鲁棒推理。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications require estimating unknown parameters from experimentally observable data -- an inverse problem that is inherently challenging due to nonlinearity, noise, and ill-posedness. In this paper, we propose an active diffusion-based inverse problem solver. A DM is trained to learn the mapping between the parameter space and the observable space. By iteratively detecting and correcting model misspecification through posterior uncertainty, the method discovers and learns the correct region of parameter space, even when initial training bounds exclude the true parameters. This provides a principled, Bayesian justification for adaptive domain augmentation and ensures robust inference for inverse problems under incomplete prior knowledge. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our inverse solver for a toy inverse problem with infinite solutions, and for the parameterization of the quantum correlation functions to event observables in a Quantum Chromodynamics analysis of nucleon structure.

</details>
